Callers still passing bare tuples (`walk_walker.py` `_compute_roi_and_observe` at line 554; `velocity_model.py` two call sites at lines 786 and 798) are WS2-C's / a follow-up's job to migrate; they will fail the new guards until updated. `walk_walker.py` also reads `obs.center_pixel` (line ~594 region) and `velocity_model.py` unpacks `bx, by = observation.center_pixel` (line 820) -- both must switch to `.cx`/`.cy` when migrated. Verified: pyflakes clean on `residual_motion.py`; `pytest -k "coord_space or bug_101"` 43 passed / 1 xfailed; `pytest -k residual` 63 passed; the 5 migrated observe test files 32 passed.
- **`_compute_roi_and_observe` migrated to the typed `observe_blob_at` boundary + off-frame in_bounds soft-miss (M2 / WS2-C): [walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py).** WS2-B changed `observe_blob_at` to require typed PROCESSED inputs and return a `coord_space.SourcePoint`, which broke the walker's bare-tuple call. The walker now builds typed PROCESSED primitives: `pred_center = ProcessedPoint(anchor_cx, anchor_cy)`, `pred_box = ProcessedBox(anchor_cx, anchor_cy, seed_w, seed_h)`, `acceptance_box` and `roi_override` as `ProcessedBox`es whose `.edges()` equal the exact pixel rectangles the walker built before (acceptance `cx +/- 0.5w`, `cy +/- 0.75h`; ROI padded by `max(20, seed_w)` and clamped to `reader.width`/`height`); `dog_diameter_override` stays the processed scalar `0.7 * seed_w`. The ROI/acceptance math stays in processed space (no re-introduced source->processed conversion of the already-processed seed inputs -- that was the reverted WS0-A regression). Off-frame is now an explicit typed predicate: before calling observe, `ProcessedPoint(anchor_cx, anchor_cy).in_bounds(reader.geometry)` is tested; when False the function logs one WARNING and returns `(None, trace_sink_holder)` -- the same soft-miss shape both callers already handle (bootstrap sets `soft_miss_no_blob` at line 766; the per-step loop feeds `obs=None` into `_build_window_entry` at line 914, whose line-633 None-handling sets `trace=None` and `candidates=[]`). This is the graceful replacement for the deep degenerate-ROI crash (bug #101): the Jason interval `[5781, 5828]` BWD off-frame frames now emit soft-misses and the walk completes (e2e `e2e_bug_101_degenerate_roi.py` now exits 0 "NO CRASH"; `test_bug_101_degenerate_roi.py` xpasses). NOTE: the walker never reads `obs.center_pixel` as a feedback anchor -- the next-step anchor comes from the Viterbi path over `trace.corridor_blobs` centroids, which are already PROCESSED-space; the only source-out value is the now-typed `BlobObservation.center_pixel: SourcePoint`, which this file does not consume, so there is no source-as-processed feedback site to convert here. WS2-D must convert the batch-path SOURCE seeds to PROCESSED so the Jason interval produces a real track instead of an all-soft-miss interval; until then the walk completes without crashing but emits per-frame off-frame warnings. New focused test `test_blob_walk_v2_offframe_softmiss.py` (2 tests: off-frame anchor returns soft-miss without calling observe; on-frame anchor calls observe with typed PROCESSED args whose box edges match the walker's pixel rectangles). Verified: pyflakes clean; `pytest -k "blob_walk_v2 or coord_space"` 190 passed.
- **[walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py): bootstrap and per-step ROI construction changed from source-to-processed `reader.geometry.source_*` clamps to direct `reader.width`/`reader.height` clamps.** Seeds from `SeedsView` are already in processed space; `reader.width`/`height` is the correct processed-frame bound. Source-frame dim lookup (`reader.geometry.source_width/height`) removed from both block.
- **`walk_driver.py`: now uses `load_walker_seeds_view` + `assert_geometry_match` instead of `load_walker_seeds`.** Processed-pixel seeds from the view are looked up by frame_index and passed to `walk_one_direction`. `enumerate_seed_to_seed_intervals` still called with `seeds_view.source` for frame-index bookkeeping.
- **`dump_step1_inputs.py`: seed coords projected to processed space at top of `dump_step1_for_interval`** via `reader.geometry.source_to_processed*`. All downstream `acceptance_box`, `roi_override`, `dog_diameter_override`, `pred_center`, `pred_box` constructions now in processed space. `has_valid_seed_roi` call site fixed to use `reader.geometry.source_width/height` (was incorrectly using post-bin `reader.width/height`).

- **Real per-frame trace threaded into the renderer (WS2-A): `walk_driver.py` + [walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py).** The empty-stub trace reconstruction was replaced with the actual per-frame `BlobObserverTrace`, exposed as `WalkSummary.direction_trace_map`. Heavy arrays (`residual_dog`, `validity_mask`) are dropped via a lighten step to bound memory. Trace geometry stays processed/ROI-local.
- **Render now draws the human seed box and the solved box (WS2-B1): `walk_render.py` + `walk_draw.py`.** The human seed box (from `SeedsView.seeds`, processed, solid + heavy) and the solved box (from `direction_path`, processed, dashed + normal) are drawn with edges derived from the float center, using a single processed->tile-local conversion (`conversion_count == 1`). `render_walk_tile` now returns a per-tile manifest dict. Fixed a stale "source frame coords" comment.
- **Blob ellipses redrawn from real trace centroids (WS2-B2): `walk_render.py`.** Winner/corridor/raw ellipses now come from the real trace centroids (processed, one ROI subtraction, no bin upscale). The acceptance box is kept as a distinct restyled amber "acceptance corridor" overlay (clearly not a torso box). The dead winner-stub path was removed.
- **Seed and solved boxes threaded through the render calls (WS2-INT): `walk_driver.py`.** Per-frame `seed_box` (from `SeedsView.seeds`) and `solved_box` (from `direction_path`) are passed into the `render_walk_tile` calls and the returned manifests collected. Render-only mode passes `solved_box=None` (npz down-projection flagged as a follow-up).
- **Render-only mode now loads and down-projects source-frame npz boxes (WS2-A follow-up): `walk_driver.py`.** When `direction_path=None` and `npz_path`/`interval_start_frame`/`interval_end_frame` are provided, `_render_direction_tiles` loads the torso_box_coords.npz via `state_io.load_torso_box_coords`, finds the matching interval by `start_frame`/`end_frame`, and down-projects the `blended_path` (source-frame pixels) to PROCESSED using `geometry.source_to_processed` (for `cx,cy`) and `geometry.source_to_processed_delta` (for `w,h`). Box math stays in float throughout (rounding deferred to draw boundary). This is exactly one source->processed conversion; `render_walk_tile` subtracts `roi_origin` once = total `conversion_count == 1`. On a missing npz or no matching interval, `solved_box` falls back to `None` (render-only with no prior walk is a supported use case, not a bug-hiding default). The `_render_direction_tiles` "Follow-up task" docstring updated to describe the implemented behavior. `run_interval_walk` gains an optional `npz_path` parameter; `main()` computes `video_npz_path` once per video and passes it to `run_interval_walk`, also reusing it for the npz write block (replacing a second `_video_npz_path(video_basename)` call).
- **Production entry point wires seed boxes into the render (WS2-C): `make_walk_html_v2.py`** now passes `proc_seed_by_frame` through so seed boxes actually render. Re-rendering the corpus with `--walk` confirmed seed + solved boxes + ellipses land on the runner for the bin=1 and bin=2 videos (no longer "magenta + only"); 4 of 6 videos rendered 596 tiles, all manifest-PASS.

### Removals and Deprecations

- **Aggressively purged the blob_walk_v2 pytest suite from 11 files to 5; the e2e baseline gate (`tests/e2e/e2e_blob_walk_baseline.sh`) already byte-checks the full walker output (per-frame status + numeric verdict columns + accepted_fraction) on 4 real intervals, so any unit test re-verifying walker internals or end-to-end runs was redundant.** Deleted via `git rm` (whole files): `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_bootstrap_radius.py` (re-implemented the gate math in the test and pinned the tunable `BOOTSTRAP_N`; real gate behavior is covered by the e2e gate and `evaluate()` math in motion_gate); `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_provisional_observation.py` (tested a removed feature via `inspect.getsource` string-grep on internal names and pinned `SCHEMA_VERSION==13` / header length 43 -- fragile per PYTEST_STYLE); `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_soft_miss.py` (only `hasattr`-absence checks, constant pinning, and re-derived gate math -- redundant); `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_synthetic_regression.py` (re-implemented the walker rolling-buffer loop inside the test and asserted exact counts the gate byte-checks on the real walker); `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_trace_sink_populated.py` (heavily stubbed "does not crash + columns exist" smoke test); `tests/test_make_walk_html_v2.py` (pure `--help`-exits-0 subprocess smoke test, forbidden by the PYTEST_STYLE runtime budget). Trimmed (kept files, fragile classes/tests removed): `test_blob_walk_v2_motion_gate.py` dropped `TestPhysicalConstants` and `TestMotionGateResult` (constant pinning + dataclass-storage), `TestDtForGateClamped` (pins `DT_GATE_CAP`), and duplicate accept/reject-reason tests; kept the distinct `evaluate()` reject-reason discriminators. `test_blob_walk_v2_windowed.py` dropped `TestSchemaVersion13`, `TestWalkerConstants`, `TestWalkSummaryFields`, the cross-frame-state `hasattr` checks, and the `_ALLOWED_STATUS` membership checks; kept the Viterbi `select_path` and `emit_status_from_path` pure-function tests on hand-built lattices (edge cases the gate's real-blob path does not exercise). `test_blob_walk_v2_debug_log.py` dropped the full-`HEADER`-tuple equality assertion and `len(rows)==1` collection-size checks; kept value round-trip, None-to-blank, and invalid-status `ValueError`. `test_blob_walk_v2_winner_modes.py` dropped the `WalkSummary` dataclass-field test; kept the four `resolve_audit_winner` rule tests (a kept feature the gate does not exercise, since it walks with `production_winner` / `audit_rule=None`). Kept whole: `test_blob_walk_v2_no_hermite.py` (C6 architectural boundary -- no other gate forbids Hermite imports). Suite total: 5 kept files, 21 tests; `e2e_blob_walk_baseline.sh` gate PASS (224 verdict rows match baseline).
- **Deleted the redundant `tools/blob_walk_v2/blob_walk_v2_cli.py` wrapper and its smoke test `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_cli.py`.** After earlier cleanup the CLI had only `render` plus an `all` alias left, and both just subprocess-called `make_walk_html_v2.py` with the same flags, making the CLI a 1-command pass-through. Removed via `git rm`; `make_walk_html_v2.py` is now the entry point (see Behavior changes). The CLI's smoke test only exercised the deleted module's `--help` and subcommand parsing, so it was replaced by `tests/test_make_walk_html_v2.py`, which asserted the entry point's `--help` exits 0 and listed the render flags (itself since removed as a redundant smoke test; see the 2026-05-29 purge entry above). The e2e harness imports `walk_driver` directly (never the CLI), so the gate is unaffected.
- **Deleted `tools/blob_walk_v2/score_corpus_quality.py` and the `score` CLI subcommand; stopped writing `corpus_quality.csv` and `QUALITY_SCORES.md`.** The scorer's quality readout now lives inside `walk.html` (see Behavior changes). Removed via `git rm`. `blob_walk_v2_cli.py` no longer registers `score`; the `all` subcommand is now a thin alias for `render` (was `score -> render`). `README.md` updated to drop the `score` subcommand row, the `corpus_quality.csv` / `QUALITY_SCORES.md` output paths, and the scorer's standalone-scripts row. Stale CLI smoke test `test_blob_walk_v2_cli.py` (since deleted) updated to the current subcommand set (it had been asserting on long-removed `dump`/`replay`/`audit` names). Reason: consolidating the quality readout into the single `walk.html` artifact per user direction; no walker or schema change.
- **Removed `test_observe_blob_at_dog_override_converted` and `test_observe_blob_at_roi_override_converted` from [test_residual_motion_bin_factor.py](../tests/test_residual_motion_bin_factor.py).** These tests verified the old Model B behavior (conversion inside `observe_blob_at`). Under Option A the conversion is the caller's responsibility; the tests would have asserted incorrect behavior. Replacement tests with the new contract live in [test_observe_blob_at_processed_contract.py](../tests/test_observe_blob_at_processed_contract.py).
- **Deleted `tools/blob_walk_v2/compare_walker_vs_hermite.py` and `tests/test_compare_walker_vs_hermite.py`.** The walker-vs-Hermite comparison premise is invalid: the walker only runs on intervals where Hermite fails, so a head-to-head comparison measures nothing meaningful. Removed via `git rm` and dropped from the blob_walk_v2 README diagnostic-scripts section.
- **Windowed path-selection amendment archived to [windowed_path_selection_amendment.md](archive/windowed_path_selection_amendment.md).** Plan closed per WP-3C of `~/.claude/plans/sequential-soaring-hopper.md`. Source location `docs/active_plans/active/windowed_path_selection_amendment.md` removed after copy-verify (file was untracked; closure used `cp` + `rm` per REPO_STYLE active-plans archive convention). A 3-paragraph `## CLOSED 2026-05-29` block prepended to the archived copy records status, primary results, and known followup #101.
- **Extraction re-scope follow-ups archived to [extraction_rescope_followups.md](archive/extraction_rescope_followups.md).** Per #91 coverage map: the E-1..E-9A ticket family is out of scope under the final windowed-walker design, which contained windowing to `tools/blob_walk_v2/walk_walker.py` and left `track_runner/residual_motion.py` untouched. Ticket E-1 is partially covered (consumer reads `BlobObserverTrace.corridor_blobs`; no formal `extract_blobs_at` API split). Source location removed after copy-verify.
- **Deleted offline model-shootout diagnostic cluster (5 files) from `tools/blob_walk_v2/` and removed `dump`, `replay`, and `audit` subcommands from `blob_walk_v2_cli.py`.** The cluster was a design-phase model-selection harness used to evaluate bootstrap motion models during the walker development phase. The acceptance milestone is closed and the walker motion model is frozen; no production code depended on any of the deleted modules. Files removed via `git rm`: `tools/blob_walk_v2/dump_initial_seed_velocity.py`, `tools/blob_walk_v2/replay_initial_seed_velocity.py`, `tools/blob_walk_v2/replay_models.py`, `tools/blob_walk_v2/sweep_radius.py`, `tests/test_replay_initial_seed_velocity_5bin.py`. CLI subcommands removed: `dump`, `replay`, `audit`; `run_all` rewritten to `score -> render` only. `tools/blob_walk_v2/README.md` updated to remove deleted scripts from all tables and examples. E2E gate passes.

### Fixes and Maintenance

- **Bug #101 fully closed: both walker entry points now feed PROCESSED seeds, and the `run_interval_walk` seed boundary is typed (typed-coord M2 / WS2-D).** Root cause recap: the walker had two entry points feeding DIFFERENT coordinate spaces. `walk_driver.main` loaded PROCESSED seeds (`load_walker_seeds_view(...).seeds`) while `make_walk_html_v2.process_video` loaded SOURCE seeds (`load_walker_seeds`) and passed source cx/cy/w/h straight into `walk_driver.run_interval_walk`. At `bin>1` the source seed (Jason frame 5781 cx=1870) built an ROI clamped against the processed width (1408); after WS2-C the deep crash became an all-soft-miss, so the batch path produced a useless walk for bin>1 videos. Fix (1): `make_walk_html_v2.py` `process_video` now mirrors `walk_driver.main` exactly -- it loads a `SeedsView` via `walk_io.load_walker_seeds_view(video_basename, reader.geometry)`, calls `seeds_view.assert_geometry_match(reader.geometry)`, enumerates intervals from `seeds_view.source` for frame-index bookkeeping only, and looks up the PROCESSED seed dict (`proc_seed_by_frame[frame_index]`, built from `seeds_view.seeds`) to pass into `run_interval_walk`. The same processed map already feeds the renderer's seed box, so walk and render now share one canonical processed-seed source. The stale `load_walker_seeds` call (source seeds) is gone. Fix (2): `walk_driver.py` `run_interval_walk` now types the seed boundary -- at the top it builds `coord_space.ProcessedBox(cx, cy, w, h)` for each seed and runs `coord_space.require_processed_box`, and the FWD/BWD `neighbor_seed_*` args are threaded from those validated boxes (`right_box.cx`, etc.) rather than re-reading the dicts. A future caller that hands in a SOURCE-derived seed is caught loud at this boundary instead of degenerating deep inside `observe_blob_at`; no source->processed conversion of seeds happens inside the walker (seeds arrive PROCESSED by contract). The docstring documents the PROCESSED-space requirement. Verification: pyflakes clean on both files; `pytest tests/ -k "blob_walk_v2 or bug_101 or coord_space"` -> 200 passed, 1 xpassed (now 201 passed after the bug_101 test was flipped to a positive assertion); real bin>1 run `make_walk_html_v2.py --walk -v Jason-3200m-sectionals-IMG_4005 --intervals-from-corpus dump_step1/24corpus/` completed with `errors=0`, no off-frame WARNING spam, and interval [5781,5828] produced REAL accepts (FWD accepted=10, BWD accepted=13, both stop_reason `hit_neighbor_seed`) instead of all-soft-miss. Observed processed seed cx for Jason frame 5781 = 935.0 (= source 1870.0 / bin 2), mid-frame and in-bounds (< processed width 1408). `tests/test_bug_101_degenerate_roi.py` rewritten from the `xfail(raises=ValueError)` reproduction into a positive `test_bug_101_degenerate_roi_no_longer_raises` that drives the original reproducing call path and asserts it completes without the degenerate-ROI raise (a regression re-flips it to a hard fail). NOTE: the e2e reproducer `reproduce_degenerate_roi()` still loads SOURCE seeds on purpose (it probes the crash path directly); it now exits 0 because off-frame is an in-bounds soft-miss, confirming the crash is gone -- the all-soft-miss cure is the `process_video` SeedsView change, exercised by the real bin>1 run above.

- **Deterministic #101 reproduction harness landed (typed-coord M0 / WS0-B, Patch 2).** Added the former `e2e_bug_101_degenerate_roi.py` non-browser E2E gate and the thin xfail pytest `tests/test_bug_101_degenerate_roi.py` (both since removed). The e2e script mirrors `make_walk_html_v2.process_video`'s BATCH wiring: it loads SOURCE-pixel seeds via `walk_io.load_walker_seeds` (NOT the processed `SeedsView`), builds the interval via `enumerate_seed_to_seed_intervals`, and calls `walk_driver.run_interval_walk` with the source seed dicts on the bin_factor=2 Jason-3200m-sectionals-IMG_4005 fixture (source 2816x1584 -> processed 1408x784), interval [5781, 5828]. It exits 1 when the degenerate-ROI `ValueError` fires (bug present), 0 when the walk completes without it (bug fixed), 2 on an unexpected error -- so the gate flips green when the M2 typed-coord fix turns off-frame into an in-bounds soft-miss. The pytest is `xfail(strict=False, raises=ValueError)`, skipped when the real source video is absent, and drives the same `reproduce_degenerate_roi()` helper. Verification: `python3 tests/e2e/e2e_bug_101_degenerate_roi.py` exits 1 with `ValueError: degenerate ROI (h=92, w=0) at frame 5781; prediction off-frame?`; `pytest tests/ -k bug_101 -v` reports `1 xfailed`. EXACT falsified call path (against the WS0-A processed-native baseline): `make_walk_html_v2.process_video` line 298 loads source seeds, line 313 builds intervals from them, lines 355-356 pass `interval.left_seed` (SOURCE-pixel: cx=1870.0, cy=674.5, w=18.0, h=35.0) into `run_interval_walk`; `walk_walker._compute_roi_and_observe` (walk_walker.py:554) builds `roi_override=(1841, 628, 1408, 720)` -- source-scale x clamped against processed width -- and calls `residual_motion.observe_blob_at` with `pred_center=(1870.0, 674.5)` (also SOURCE); inside observe, the override-clamp (residual_motion.py:1165-1168) collapses the x-range to `[1408, 1408]` (ox1==ox2==reader.width) -> `compute_residual_for_frame` crops a zero-width array and raises at residual_motion.py:589. `roi_override` is NOT None (the original `roi_override=None` else-branch hypothesis is FALSIFIED); the trigger is the SOURCE-as-PROCESSED scale mismatch on the bootstrap-step observe. Batch crashes because `process_video` feeds source seeds; `walk_driver.main()` feeds processed seeds from `SeedsView.seeds` (walk_driver.py:938), which is why an isolated processed-seed `run_interval_walk` ran clean. WS0-A's revert did not change this trigger -- it removed a separate double-conversion regression, leaving the batch source-seed scale mismatch intact (the durable fix is M2). `pyflakes` clean on both new files.

- **Reverted the in-session "bin fix" regression in the blob walker; restored the clean processed-native baseline (WS0-A).** The earlier same-day change (the two entries below describing `_compute_acceptance_and_roi` and the degenerate-ROI soft-miss guard) wrongly treated the walker's already-processed inputs as SOURCE and converted them source->processed a second time, double-dividing by `bin_factor` and mislocating every walker ROI at `bin>1` (proven: a processed `pred_center` of (935, 337) produced an `roi_override` centered at ~467 == 935/2 at bin=2). The walk pipeline operates entirely in PROCESSED (post-bin) pixels: the driver feeds processed seeds and `residual_motion.observe_blob_at` consumes processed `pred_center`/`roi_override`/`dog_diameter_override`/`acceptance_box` (Option A). Revert details: deleted the `_compute_acceptance_and_roi` helper from [walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py); restored `_compute_roi_and_observe` to build `acceptance_box`, `roi_pad`, `roi_x1..roi_y2`, and `dog_diameter_override = 0.7 * seed_w` directly from the already-processed `anchor_cx`/`anchor_cy`/`seed_w`/`seed_h` (no source->processed conversion), clamping against `reader.width`/`reader.height`; removed the degenerate-ROI soft-miss guard and the now-unused `import logging`/`logger`; restored the `in_acceptance_box` trace-enrichment branch in [residual_motion.py](../track_runner/residual_motion.py) (~line 1356) to its pre-session form (applies `source_to_processed` when `bin_factor != 1`); deleted the regression test `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_roi_bin.py`. KEPT untouched: the WS1-A/WS2-A work (`conf_from_anchor`, `make_size_at_frame`, `lighten_trace`, `WalkSummary.direction_path`/`direction_trace_map`, `neighbor_seed_w`/`h` params) and the render-only-npz change in `walk_driver._render_direction_tiles`. The production acceptance-box filter (~line 1284) and the degenerate-ROI raise (~line 588) in `residual_motion.py` are unchanged. Verification: pyflakes clean on both edited files; `pytest tests/ -k blob_walk_v2` -> 155 passed; throwaway proof confirmed the rebuilt `roi_override` for processed `pred_center` (935, 337) at bin=2 is centered at (935.0, 337.0), not 467.

- **Off-frame prediction in `_compute_roi_and_observe` ([walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py)) now degrades to a soft miss instead of crashing the interval.** When the BWD pass drifts far past the frame edge (observed on Jason-3200m interval [5781,5828], source 2816x1584, bin=4, source cx ~3400 -> processed ~850 >> post-bin width 704), the clamped ROI collapses to zero width (`roi_x2 <= roi_x1`), which previously propagated into `observe_blob_at` and raised `ValueError: degenerate ROI` (residual_motion.py:588), crashing the whole interval. Fix: added a degenerate-ROI guard in `_compute_roi_and_observe` that fires immediately after `_compute_acceptance_and_roi` returns, before any call to `observe_blob_at`. When `roi_x2 <= roi_x1 or roi_y2 <= roi_y1`, the function logs one WARNING (frame, source anchor, seed size, post-bin bounds, degenerate roi tuple) and returns `(None, holder)` as a soft miss. The ValueError in `residual_motion.py:588` is untouched and continues to guard non-walker callers. `import logging` and module-level `logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)` added to `walk_walker.py`. New test `test_offframe_anchor_returns_none_no_crash` added to the now-deleted `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_roi_bin.py` (source cx=3400 at bin=4, 2816x1584 source -> 704x396 post-bin, produces degenerate roi_override, asserts obs is None and holder has observer_trace). `pytest tests/ -k blob_walk_v2` -> 159 passed. NOTE: this entry describes an in-session change that was later reverted (see the WS0-A revert entry above); the referenced test file no longer exists.

- **Fixed #101 degenerate ROI (`w=0`) at `bin_factor>1` during the walk: [walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py) `_compute_roi_and_observe` was building `acceptance_box`, `roi_override`, and `dog_diameter_override` from SOURCE-frame anchor/seed values, then clamping the ROI against POST-BIN `reader.width`/`height`.** At `bin_factor=4` a source `cx` near the right edge (e.g. ~3600) clamped against post-bin width ~960 produced `roi_x1 > roi_x2`; `observe_blob_at` collapsed that to zero width and raised `ValueError: degenerate ROI`. Root cause: the inputs were SOURCE but the function's comment claimed PROCESSED (Option A). Fix: new pure helper `_compute_acceptance_and_roi(cx_src, cy_src, seed_w_src, seed_h_src, geometry, frame_w, frame_h)` converts the anchor center via `geometry.source_to_processed` and the torso size via `geometry.source_to_processed_delta`, then builds the three caller-converted kwargs (`acceptance_box`, `roi_override`, `dog_diameter_override = 0.7 * w_p`) and clamps against the now space-consistent post-bin `reader.width`/`height`. CRITICAL: `pred_center=(anchor_cx, anchor_cy)` and `pred_box=(seed_w, seed_h)` remain SOURCE in the `observe_blob_at` call (observe converts those itself); only the three override kwargs switch to PROCESSED. Also removed a stale pre-Option-A `source_to_processed` branch from the `observe_blob_at` trace-enrichment block in [residual_motion.py](../track_runner/residual_motion.py) (~line 1356) that double-shifted `in_acceptance_box` trace metadata at `bin_factor>1`; it now reads `acceptance_box` directly, matching the production filter (~line 1284). Trace/diagnostic metadata only; no production data-flow change, `observe_blob_at`'s contract and the production acceptance-box filter are untouched. New regression test `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_roi_bin.py` (3 property checks: non-degenerate + in-bounds ROI at bin=4 near the source right/bottom edge, processed-scale center, bin=1 sanity) via a stub geometry; NOTE: this test file was later deleted in the WS0-A revert (see entry above) and no longer exists. Verification: pyflakes clean on both files + the test; `pytest tests/ -k blob_walk_v2` -> 158 passed; `pytest tests/test_tr_state_io.py` -> 13 passed.

- **`tools/blob_walk_v2/` imports brought into [PYTHON_STYLE.md](PYTHON_STYLE.md) compliance.** Pure import-style refactor; byte-identical walker output, no schema change. Changed: `from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict` -> `import dataclasses` with qualified call sites (`@dataclasses.dataclass`, `dataclasses.asdict()`); `from typing import Optional` deleted; all `Optional[T]` annotations replaced with `T | None` builtin syntax; `import typing` removed from `walk_util.py` with `typing.Optional[float]` replaced by `float | None`; test `from walk_motion_gate import evaluate` replaced by `import walk_motion_gate` with `walk_motion_gate.evaluate(` at all call sites. Files changed: [walk_debug_log.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_debug_log.py), [walk_motion_gate.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_motion_gate.py), `walk_html.py`, `walk_util.py`, `test_blob_walk_v2_motion_gate.py`. All pyflakes clean; 315 tests pass; `e2e_blob_walk_baseline.sh` gate PASS (224 verdict rows unchanged).

- **Removed backward-compat alias shims from [walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py).** The six constant shims (`WEIGHT_DISPLACEMENT`, `WEIGHT_MAG_VAR`, `WEIGHT_ANGLE_VAR`, `WEIGHT_EVIDENCE`, `SKIP_COST`, `EXTRAP_MAX`) and two function alias shims (`_viterbi_select_path`, `_emit_status_from_path`) that re-pointed to canonical names in `walk_viterbi` and `walk_status` are deleted. Tests in `test_blob_walk_v2_windowed.py` and `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_synthetic_regression.py` (since removed) now import `walk_viterbi` and `walk_status` directly and reference the canonical names (`walk_viterbi.select_path`, `walk_status.emit_status_from_path`, `walk_viterbi.SKIP_COST`, `walk_viterbi.WEIGHT_*`, `walk_status.EXTRAP_MAX`). Native `walk_walker` names (`WALKER_WINDOW_FRAMES`, `BOOTSTRAP_N`, `WalkSummary`, `resolve_audit_winner`) are unchanged. No arithmetic or logic changed; byte-identical walker output confirmed by `e2e_blob_walk_baseline.sh` gate PASS. No schema change.

- **`walk_walker.walk_one_direction` split into single-purpose helpers; Viterbi and status logic lifted into new modules.** Pure structural refactor; zero behavior change. The Viterbi path-selection trio (`_viterbi_select_path`, `_transition_cost`, `_compute_path_cost`) moved verbatim into new [walk_viterbi.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_viterbi.py) as `select_path`/`transition_cost`/`compute_path_cost`; the per-frame status trio (`_emit_status_from_path`, `_interp_or_extrap_position`, `_count_candidates_in_window`) moved verbatim into new [walk_status.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_status.py). Viterbi cost weights (`SKIP_COST`, `WEIGHT_*`) now live in `walk_viterbi`; the extrapolation cap (`EXTRAP_MAX`) lives in `walk_status`; [walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py) keeps compatibility bindings so those constants and the two test-referenced functions stay reachable as `walk_walker.<name>` for diagnostic tools and tests. `walk_one_direction` is now an orchestrator calling `_run_bootstrap_step`, `_run_windowed_steps`, `_emit_diagnostic_rows`, and `_compute_summary_metrics` in the same order with the same data flow; the duplicated ROI-construct + clamp + `observe_blob_at` block (bootstrap and per-step) is de-duplicated into `_compute_roi_and_observe`, and the buffer-entry assembly into `_build_window_entry`. No arithmetic was reordered or reassociated; whole statement blocks moved verbatim. `resolve_audit_winner` and the `WalkSummary` dataclass remain in `walk_walker`. No schema change (`walk_debug_log.SCHEMA_VERSION` unchanged); FWD/BWD passes remain independent (C9). Byte-identical walker output: `e2e_blob_walk_baseline.sh` gate PASS (224 verdict rows match baseline).

- **Draw primitives moved from `walk_render.py` to new `walk_draw.py`.** Pure structural refactor: `_compute_thickness_from_torso_h`, `_draw_dashed_circle`, `_draw_velocity_vector`, `_draw_allowed_jump_circle`, `_draw_residual_line`, `_draw_blob_ellipse_cv2`, and `_draw_plus_marker` moved verbatim (no arithmetic or cv2 call-order changes). `render_walk_tile` retains orchestration only; call sites updated to `walk_draw._draw_*(...)`. Byte-identical output; no schema change. `e2e_blob_walk_baseline.sh` gate passes.

- **`walk_util.py` extraction: moved `_to_float`, `_to_int`, `_to_float_or_none`, and `_evenly_spread` into a shared module.** Pure refactor; zero behavior change. Callers updated: `walk_driver.py` (already using `walk_util` calls; no local defs to remove), `make_walk_html_v2.py` (deleted local `_evenly_spread`, added `import walk_util`, routed call to `walk_util._evenly_spread`), `walk_html.py` (deleted local `_to_float_or_none`, added `import walk_util`, 8 call sites updated to `walk_util._to_float_or_none`). The two `_evenly_spread` copies differed only by one internal comment line; logic was identical; collapsed to one copy in `walk_util.py`. Gate: `e2e_blob_walk_baseline.sh` PASS.

- **Fixed ROI_CLAMP_SPACE_MISMATCH in [walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py)** - bootstrap (lines 812-813) and per-step (lines 900-901) blocks clamped source-coord `acceptance_box` upper bounds against `reader.width`/`reader.height` (post-bin), inverting the ROI for seeds with `cx > 960` source pixels at `bin_factor=4`. Per [degenerate_roi_investigation.md](archive/degenerate_roi_investigation.md), this was the 3rd distinct auto-bin coord bug; explains 59/120 degenerate-ROI errors in #95 v2. Fix: clamp against `reader.geometry.source_width`/`source_height` (no-op at bin=1). Two new tests added to [test_residual_motion_bin_factor.py](../tests/test_residual_motion_bin_factor.py): `test_roi_clamp_space_mismatch_fix_bin4_right_of_center` and `test_roi_clamp_noop_at_bin1`.

- **`walk_driver.py`: FWD and BWD render-loop duplication collapsed into `_render_direction_tiles`.** The two near-identical inline loops in `run_interval_walk` differed only by direction-specific values (label, csv path, tile dir, anchor seed, direction sign default). Both loops extracted verbatim into private helper `_render_direction_tiles(direction_label, csv_path, tile_dir, anchor_seed, default_direction_sign, reader, scene_transform, fps)`; `run_interval_walk` replaced with two calls. Byte-identical output; no schema change; `e2e_blob_walk_baseline.sh` gate passes.

- **Removed two PYTHON_STYLE defensive-default smells from `walk_driver.py`.** `existing["solved_intervals"]` is now accessed directly (the key must exist) instead of via a `.get()` fallback, and a missing-center check uses an explicit `in (None, '')` test instead of `not .get(...)` so a zero-valued center is not falsely treated as missing.
- **`README.md` updated** for the walker-solved torso-box path, the per-video npz persistence, and the render-manifest gate.
- **`test_blob_walk_v2_no_hermite.py`: `walk_driver.py` whitelisted** as the orchestration layer (it uses `interval_solver.blend_paths`, which is not the walker algorithm), so the C6 Hermite-import scan no longer flags it.

### Decisions and Failures

- **Windowed path-selection walker plan closed; both primary acceptance bars PASS on two corpus runs.** #85 24-corpus rerun: FWD accepted_fraction 42.3%, BWD 41.0% across 48 walks (bars FWD >= 34.7%, BWD >= 24.6%). #95 v2 120-corpus rerun (after #100 auto-bin scale-factor fix): FWD 38.7%, BWD 39.1% across 61 valid intervals of 120 (5 of 6 videos), at ~5x wall-clock speedup from auto-bin at bin_factor=4. Delta vs the #78 per-frame cascade-fix baseline on the 24-corpus: FWD +22.6pp, BWD +31.4pp. M3 acceptance bar marked PASS in the M0 closure summary update.
- **Coord-system robustness design recorded at [coord_system_robustness_design.md](archive/coord_system_robustness_design.md). Recommendation: Option A (geometry-aware `state_io.load_seeds`).** Three distinct auto-bin coord defects shipped between 2026-05-28 and 2026-05-29 (WARP_SCALE_MISMATCH, DoG/roi_override source-scale, ROI_CLAMP_SPACE_MISMATCH) all sit at the seed/walker -> reader/observer boundary. Per REPO_STYLE "fix the design, not the symptom," patching a fourth boundary would extend the pattern. Option A localizes conversion to a single load-time site and makes the walker pipeline coord-uniform with a small refactor (~6 files). Option B (tagged Coord type) deferred as future move if a 4th defect surfaces. Scene coords stay distinct. Design-only; no code edits in this entry.
- **Known followup #101 (filter/clamp divergence on degenerate ROI).** All 20 Lyra-Wheeling intervals in the v2 120-corpus failed with `degenerate ROI (h=0, w=0)` errors raised by `compute_residual_for_frame`. Dump-time filter `has_valid_seed_roi` accepts these seeds at source-pixel resolution; render-time clamp in `compute_residual_for_frame` produces zero area when prediction lands off-frame. Logged as a separate ticket; does not affect the closed amendment's primary acceptance bars.
- **WS1-C walker npz coordinate contract proven and recorded at `docs/active_plans/decisions/walker_npz_coord_contract.md` (hard gate for WS1-B / WS2-B1).** Proven from code: (a) `state_io.write/load_torso_box_coords` are PRESERVE-ONLY (round+clip to uint16, no crop/bin/ROI transform), so the walker owns the processed->source projection; (b) the projection reuses existing `FrameGeometry.processed_to_source` / `processed_to_source_delta` (inverse of `SeedsView._build_processed`), no hand-rolled math; (c) write path = `tr_paths.default_intervals_path` -> `tr_config/{stem}.track_runner.torso_box_coords.npz`, one shared npz per video keyed by `compute_interval_fingerprint`, load-merge-write-back-once, aggregate-before-write for full corpus; reader = `cli.py` `_predictions_from_torso_box_coords` / `_load_prior_results`; (d) `roi_origin_xy` is PROCESSED-frame, NO separate crop origin exists (`FrameGeometry` is origin-preserving, crop=0), canonical `tile = coord - crop - roi`; (e) C10 additive -- one unified `SCHEMA_VERSION=11`, no new constant, no bump. Asymmetric-sentinel round-trip verified (bin=4, source 3840x2178, ROI (37,91), center not bin-divisible, w!=h): processed (213.5,401.25,46,73) -> source (854,1605,184,292) -> npz reload (854,1605,184,292), exact. CONCERN flagged: the plan's "canonical render space = source-frame" is wrong for the rendered TILE -- at bin>1 the walker tile is PROCESSED-frame (tile is a crop of binned `reader.read_frame`), so WS2-B1 must land its single source-to-tile conversion in processed space; the npz contract (source-frame) is unaffected. Audit only; no production code edits.

- **Render-only mode (no `--walk`) currently passes `solved_box=None`.** The npz->processed down-projection needed to draw the solved box in render-only mode is a documented follow-up, not part of this change. The `--walk` path draws the solved box directly from the in-memory `direction_path`.
- **Pre-existing followup #101 (degenerate ROI `w=0` at bin>1 during the walk, [residual_motion.py](../track_runner/residual_motion.py):590) blocks rendering of Conant and Lyra-Wheeling (bin=4) and some bin=2 intervals.** This is NOT caused by the walker-solves-the-interval work; it is the same off-frame degenerate-ROI clamp logged under #101 above. Flagged for a separate ticket. The 2 of 6 videos that did not render are blocked solely by this issue.
- **Verification for the walker-solves-the-interval plan.** The coordinate sentinel passed 4/4 (asymmetric bin=4 / ROI(37,91) / `w != h` through processed->source->npz->reload->tile). `pytest tests -k blob_walk_v2` reports 155 passed. The render-manifest gate passed on all 596 rendered tiles across the 4 renderable videos. WS1-C audit confirmed `SCHEMA_VERSION` is additive (no bump) and that `state_io` is preserve-only, so the walker owns the processed->source projection.

## 2026-05-28

### Fixes and Maintenance

- **Fixed auto-bin warp scale-factor mismatch + override-args source-scale ([residual_motion.py](../track_runner/residual_motion.py) production path and `observe_blob_at`).** Defects cataloged in [auto_bin_coord_stack_audit.md](archive/auto_bin_coord_stack_audit.md). Fix 1 (WARP_SCALE_MISMATCH): `compute_residual_for_frame` production warp path now passes `scale_factor = 1/bin_factor` instead of hard-coded `1.0`; at `bin_factor=4` the warp translation was 4x too large, collapsing the residual map and producing `obs_corridor_n=0` on every frame. Fix 2 (DOG_DIAMETER + ROI_OVERRIDE source-scale): `observe_blob_at` now converts `dog_diameter_override` and `roi_override` from source-pixel units to processed-pixel units via `geometry.source_to_processed_delta` / `geometry.source_to_processed` before use. Auto-bin re-enabled in `walk_io.py`; walker now produces non-empty residual and non-zero `corridor_blobs` under `bin_factor>1`. 24-corpus / 120-corpus reruns can use auto-bin.

### Developer Tests and Notes

- **4 new tests in [test_residual_motion_bin_factor.py](../tests/test_residual_motion_bin_factor.py)** verify warp scale_factor, dog_diameter_override conversion, roi_override conversion, and non-empty residual output at bin_factor=4; all pass. Pyflakes clean on all changed files.

### Behavior or Interface Changes

- **`--per-video-n` flag added to `dump_step1_inputs.py` and forwarded through `blob_walk_v2_cli.py` (since deleted) `dump` subcommand.** `--per-video-n N` controls intervals-per-video in `--random-corpus` mode (default 4). Output subdir is now `<N * nvideos>corpus` computed at runtime (backward-compatible: n=4, 6 videos -> `24corpus` as before). Also added `has_valid_seed_roi(interval, frame_width, frame_height)` to pre-screen intervals whose seed boxes produce degenerate clamped ROIs, and a degenerate-ROI skip guard in the step-N loop of `dump_step1_for_interval` so off-frame predictions at non-seed frames are recorded as None rather than crashing. 120-corpus run with `--per-video-n 20` produced 122 JSON files (61 FWD + 61 BWD): Conant=12 (6 intervals; 14 dropped off-frame), IMG_3823=40, IMG_3830=40, Jason=20 (10 intervals; 10 dropped), Lyra-Hersey=10 (5 intervals; 15 dropped), Lyra-Wheeling=0 (all 20 intervals dropped off-frame; video fully excluded from corpus).

### Additions and New Features

- **Added `compare_walker_vs_hermite.py`: WP-3B comparison framework computing primary plausibility metrics (9-frame velocity-magnitude plausibility, velocity-magnitude variance, velocity-angle spread), FWD/BWD agreement, final displacement; classifies each interval walker_better / hermite_better / tie / inconclusive per amendment Section 10 multi-metric rule (tolerance=0.05 abs). Hermite trajectory loaded from `<interval_dir>/hermite_trajectory.csv` when present; with `--hermite-only` flag falls back to linear-interpolation approximation between seed pixel positions; otherwise marks interval inconclusive. Reuses helpers from `score_corpus_quality.py` (since removed): `_find_verdicts_csv`, `_load_verdicts`, `_compute_walk_metrics`, `_compute_fwd_bwd_agreement`, `_parse_seed_dir_name`. Output `SOLVER_COMPARISON.md` has four sections: aggregate classification counts, per-video tally, full per-interval metrics table, verdict against amendment credibility bar (>=50% walker_better) and quality bar. 4 classifier unit tests added in `test_compare_walker_vs_hermite.py`; all pass.**

- **Auto-bin selection function `select_bin_factor_for_analysis` added to [frame_reader.py](../common_tools/frame_reader.py); walker batch entry point `walk_io.py` updated to call it.** Adds `select_bin_factor_for_analysis(source_width, target_width=960) -> int` as a pure policy function that selects the smallest power-of-two bin_factor keeping post-bin width <= `TARGET_ANALYSIS_WIDTH_PX` (960 px). Algorithm: `next_pow2_ceil(max(1, int(source_width / target_width)))`. Examples: 3840->4, 1920->2, 1280->2 (640 px post-bin), 640->1. `FrameReader` mechanism is unchanged; no `auto_bin` flag added (policy/mechanism separation by design). `walk_io.open_walker_reader` is the single ownership site for walker auto-bin: calls the selector once per video and passes `bin_factor` to `FrameReader`. Coordinate-system model B documented in module docstring: callers use SOURCE-FRAME pixel coords; `observe_blob_at` owns source<->processed conversion internally via `FrameGeometry`; motion_track dx/dy are in source-pixel units (camera_motion rescales after phase correlation). 7 tests in `test_select_bin_factor.py` pass; pyflakes clean on frame_reader.py and walk_io.py; 105/105 blob_walk_v2 tests pass.

- **Window-level path-selection redesign of [walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py) + schema bump to v13 in [walk_debug_log.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_debug_log.py).** Replaces per-frame single-winner model with 9-frame rolling buffer + Viterbi DP path-selection over `trace.corridor_blobs` candidate lists. Key changes: (1) Walker no longer calls `obs.center_pixel`; reads `trace.corridor_blobs` as the per-frame candidate list. (2) `WALKER_WINDOW_FRAMES = 9` rolling buffer; no emission during fill phase. (3) Viterbi DP with `WEIGHT_DISPLACEMENT=1.0`, `WEIGHT_EVIDENCE=-0.05`, `SKIP_COST=2.0`; displacement cap from `walk_motion_gate.MAX_RUNNER_SPEED_W_PER_S` in torso-width units per contract C2. (4) Full WP-1C status enum emitted: `accepted`, `interpolated`, `extrapolated`, `soft_miss_no_blob`, `soft_miss_no_path`. (5) DELETED: `production_winner`/`audit_winner` branching, `last_provisional_*` state vars, `consec_miss_no_blob` counter, per-step `walk_motion_gate.evaluate` call, velocity rolling regression, `rejected_motion_gate` emission. (6) WalkSummary gains `accepted_count`, `interpolated_count`, `extrapolated_count`, `soft_miss_no_blob_count`, `soft_miss_no_path_count`, `longest_no_accept_streak`, `accepted_fraction`, `last_accepted_frame_index`, `final_displacement_to_neighbor_px`. Schema v13: deleted `torso_w_drift_frac` (unused); added `path_cost` and `candidates_in_window`; status enum updated; legacy `rejected_motion_gate` parseable via `ALL_KNOWN_STATUS`. History entry added to [TR_SCHEMA_VERSION_HISTORY.md](TR_SCHEMA_VERSION_HISTORY.md). 32 new tests in `test_blob_walk_v2_windowed.py`; existing provisional, winner-modes, and debug-log tests updated for v13. 105/105 blob_walk_v2 tests pass; pyflakes clean.

- **New consolidated entry point `blob_walk_v2_cli.py` (since deleted) with six subcommands: `dump`, `replay`, `score`, `render`, `audit`, `all`.** Addresses repeated user request for a single driver script instead of six standalone scripts. Uses subprocess to proxy each subcommand to its existing standalone script (all four scripts call `parse_args()` internally via `sys.argv`, so subprocess is the correct glue pattern). Defaults: `--output-dir dump_step1/24corpus`, `--corpus-root blob_walk_v2/24corpus`. New `README.md` documents the entry point and marks the standalone scripts as deprecated-in-place. 3 new smoke tests in `test_blob_walk_v2_cli.py` (since deleted): `test_help_returns_zero`, `test_dump_help_returns_zero`, `test_all_subcommands_registered`. pyflakes clean; 73/73 blob_walk_v2 tests pass.

### Developer Tests and Notes

- **Added WP-3A synthetic-interval regression suite in `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_synthetic_regression.py` (since removed): clean walk, 3-frame detector gap, 10-frame trailing detector loss; all assert exact integer counts and stop_reason=hit_neighbor_seed under windowed walker.** Tests drive `_viterbi_select_path` and `_emit_status_from_path` directly through `_simulate_interval_walk`, a module-level helper that replicates the walker's rolling-buffer + Viterbi + flush loop without requiring a real VideoReader. Test 1 (clean walk): 30 frames, all accepted, accepted_fraction==1.0. Test 2 (3-frame detector gap): frames 12-14 have empty corridor_blobs; asserts accepted_count==27, soft_miss_no_blob_count==3 (empty frames produce soft_miss_no_blob per _emit_status_from_path semantics, not interpolated), accepted_fraction==27/30. Test 3 (10-frame trailing loss): frames 20-29 empty; asserts accepted_count==20, extrapolated_count+soft_miss_no_blob_count==10, accepted_fraction==20/30. All 3 pass; pyflakes clean; 108/108 blob_walk_v2 tests pass.

### Fixes and Maintenance

- **Deleted three underscore-prefixed scratch files from repo root.** `_per_video.py` (one-off 24-corpus per-video CSV aggregator), `_test_names.py` (one-off test-name lister for test_blob_walk_v2_windowed.py), and `_walker_obs.py` (one-off grep helper for walk_walker.py observation calls). All three were untracked, had no callers in tracked code, and qualify as deletable scratch under repo convention.

- **Schema v13 consumer fix: updated `walk_driver.py`, `walk_html.py`, `walk_render.py`, and [overlay_styles.yaml](../track_runner/overlay_styles.yaml) to reflect the v13 5-status enum.** Changes: (1) `walk_driver.py` `renderable_statuses` set updated -- removed stale `rejected_motion_gate`, `miss_no_blob`, `miss_low_conf`; added `interpolated`, `extrapolated`, `soft_miss_no_path`. (2) `walk_html.py` `_STATUS_BORDER_COLORS` extended with `interpolated` (#60b4e8 pale blue), `extrapolated` (#e8c840 yellow), `soft_miss_no_path` (#e87060 pale red), terminal markers `hit_neighbor_seed`/`boundary`; `_NO_TILE_STATUSES` extended with `soft_miss_no_path`, `interpolated`, `extrapolated`, `hit_neighbor_seed`, `boundary`; `walkable_statuses` in `_compute_walk_quality` extended with new v13 statuses. (3) `walk_render.py` `rejected_motion_gate` branch commented as legacy-v12-backward-compat; residual line check extended to include `soft_miss_no_path` (candidate present but no plausible path survived). (4) `overlay_styles.yaml` new `walk_svg_overlays.status_colors` block with all 5 primary statuses, terminal markers, and legacy v12 fallbacks. 105/105 blob_walk_v2 tests pass; pyflakes clean on all four files.
- **Direction-aware hotfix for provisional-observation comparison in [walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py): replace frame_index comparison with step-counter comparison.** Bug: the original cascade-fix used `last_provisional_frame > last_accepted_frame` to decide whether to anchor the next ROI to the provisional position. This is direction-naive: for BWD walks frame_index decreases, so a rejected candidate at frame 1418 (step 2) has `last_provisional_frame=1418` vs `last_accepted_frame=1419` (step 1), and 1418 > 1419 is FALSE -- provisional anchor never activates in BWD. Evidence: Conant 1389-1420 BWD row 4 (frame 1418, step 2, `status=rejected_motion_gate`, candidate exists) showed `provisional_cx_px` EMPTY and `roi_anchor_source="accepted"` instead of provisional. Fix: replaced `last_provisional_frame` / `last_accepted_frame` with monotonic step counters `last_provisional_step` / `last_accepted_step`. Comparison is now `last_provisional_step > last_accepted_step`, which is direction-safe for both FWD and BWD. FWD behavior is unchanged (step counters are equivalent to frame ordering in FWD). 4 new tests added to `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_provisional_observation.py` (since removed) in `TestBwdProvisionalActivation` class verifying: direction-naive comparison absent, step-counter comparison present, `last_provisional_step = step` assigned on reject, `last_accepted_step = step` updated on accept. pyflakes clean; 69/69 blob_walk_v2 tests pass (21/21 provisional tests pass).
- **Provisional-observation anti-freeze fix in [walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py) and [walk_debug_log.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_debug_log.py) breaks H4 velocity-freeze cascade.** Dominant failure mode: gate rejects one visible runner candidate, `prev_cx/cy` freezes at last accept, velocity stays stale, acceptance-box drifts away from runner, all remaining frames are `soft_miss_no_blob` (Conant 1389-1420 BWD: 28 consecutive misses after 1 reject). Fix: added per-walk-local provisional state `last_provisional_cx/cy/frame`. When `observe_blob_at` returns a winner blob but the motion gate rejects it, the candidate position is recorded as provisional. On the next step, if `last_provisional_frame > last_accepted_frame`, the ROI and acceptance-box are anchored to the provisional position (keeping the walker centered near the visible runner) instead of the stale last-accept. Velocity history (`accept_frames_and_scenes`, `vx_recent_scene`, `vy_recent_scene`) and accepted-count are unchanged: provisional positions never enter velocity regression. New `roi_anchor_source` field in each debug-log row records which anchor was used (`"accepted"`, `"provisional"`, `"extrapolated"`). Walker CSV schema bumped to v12; `SCHEMA_VERSION = 12` in `walk_debug_log.py` per contract C10; history entry added to [TR_SCHEMA_VERSION_HISTORY.md](TR_SCHEMA_VERSION_HISTORY.md). No constant tuning; no changes to `walk_motion_gate.py`, `residual_motion.py`, or track_runner modules. All 65 existing blob_walk_v2 tests pass; 17 new tests added in `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_provisional_observation.py` (since removed).

### Behavior or Interface Changes

- **Added "Windowed path-selection walker" section to [TRACK_RUNNER_DESIGN.md](TRACK_RUNNER_DESIGN.md) documenting the new walker model that supersedes per-frame single-winner.** Inserted after the "Anti-pattern: chained blob state" subsection inside "Signal hierarchy"; covers motivation (M0 50% frame-1 acceptance failure, leg-vs-torso `integrated_mag` oscillation), mechanism (9-frame rolling `corridor_blobs` buffer, Viterbi DP with torso-unit displacement cap, velocity/angle variance, evidence bonus, skip cost), the five-value status enum (`accepted`, `interpolated`, `extrapolated`, `soft_miss_no_blob`, `soft_miss_no_path`), contract compliance (C2, C5, C6, C9, C10), and what changed in walker state (per-frame gate removed, provisional anchor removed, hard-stop `rejected_*` statuses removed, no `last_blob`/chain-memory). Cross-links the amendment doc and the M0 closure summary.
- **Renamed `tools/blob_walk_v2/run.py` to `blob_walk_v2_cli.py` (since deleted); renamed `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_run_cli.py` to `test_blob_walk_v2_cli.py` (since deleted).** Per user feedback: dislike single-word filenames that can be confused. README + smoke-test paths updated; 3/3 tests pass.

### Decisions and Failures

- **Patched [windowed_path_selection_amendment.md](archive/windowed_path_selection_amendment.md) -- `observe_blob_at` API stays unchanged; walker reads `trace.corridor_blobs` (smaller blast radius).** Rationale from #80 touchpoint scout audit at [window_level_touchpoint_map.md](archive/window_level_touchpoint_map.md): 4 call sites to `observe_blob_at` (FWD/BWD blob snap in `velocity_model.py`, walker bootstrap, walker per-step) all classified Signature-only impact if the return stays a single `BlobObservation`; existing `BlobObserverTrace.corridor_blobs` already exposes the geometric-ROI-filtered candidate list the walker needs. Scope of windowed selection is now contained to `tools/blob_walk_v2/walk_walker.py`; `track_runner/residual_motion.py` is UNTOUCHED. Amendment Section 1, Scope, Architecture boundaries, Section 8 test plan, and Section 9 migration path updated; WALKER_WINDOW_FRAMES=9, Viterbi DP, 5-status enum, acceptance bars (FWD>=34.7%, BWD>=24.6%), and no-fallback-flag stance preserved intact.
- **Plan amendment proposed: windowed path-selection redesign for the blob walker, written at [windowed_path_selection_amendment.md](archive/windowed_path_selection_amendment.md).** Motivation: per-frame single-winner selection in `observe_blob_at` cannot disambiguate torso from leg/foot blobs on `integrated_mag` alone; M0 frame-1 acceptance bar failed 4 of 6 audit videos and 24-corpus `accepted_fraction` is 19.7%/9.6% FWD/BWD. Amendment redesigns the walker around a rolling 9-frame candidate-list buffer with Viterbi-style DP path-selection, emits the full WP-1C status enum (`accepted`, `interpolated`, `extrapolated`, `soft_miss_no_blob`, `soft_miss_no_path`), bumps `walk_debug_log` SCHEMA_VERSION per contract C10, removes the single-winner code path without a fallback flag, and pins a 15-percentage-point 24-corpus acceptance bar with an explicit rollback condition. No production code changed; plan-only.
- **M0 milestone closed.** Authoritative closure one-pager written at `m0_closure_summary.md`: dominant failure was NOT heat-map collapse but a combination of over-gated extraction (observable contract mismatch) and walker control flow killing intervals after a few early rejects or misses; both fixed via WP-1A bootstrap mode and WP-1C soft-miss walker; #70 walk.html shows 48/48 walks reach `hit_neighbor_seed`; remaining low-`accepted_fraction` on radial-motion intervals is a physical signal limitation, not a walker bug; M1 quality scoring and task #72 (HEVC caching) are the next milestones.

### Developer Tests and Notes

- **New tool `score_corpus_quality.py` (since removed) scores all 24 corpus intervals from existing fwd_verdicts.csv/bwd_verdicts.csv; no video decode.** Computes per-walk: accepted_fraction, longest_no_accept_streak, 9-frame windowed velocity-magnitude plausibility (5-20 mph @ torso_w=12in -> 7.33-29.33 W/s), windowed speed median/p95, windowed velocity-magnitude variance median, windowed velocity-angle spread median/p95, FWD/BWD per-frame Euclidean agreement (median+p95). Classifies each interval: no_signal (both FWD+BWD accepted_fraction < 0.1), completed_low_quality (either < 0.3), completed_normal (both >= 0.3). Outputs corpus_quality.csv (24 rows) and dump_step1/24corpus/QUALITY_SCORES.md. Results: no_signal=6, completed_low_quality=14, completed_normal=4. Median accepted_fraction (combined, non-no-signal)=0.255; median FWD/BWD agreement=1.010 px; median windowed_speed_in_range_fraction=0.750. No-signal dominated by Lyra-Wheeling (3 intervals) and Jason (2 intervals); Lyra-Hersey [10441-10500] also no_signal. Run: `source source_me.sh && python3 tools/blob_walk_v2/score_corpus_quality.py --corpus-root blob_walk_v2/24corpus --dump-root dump_step1/24corpus`.

- **24-corpus walk.html re-rendered at `walk.html` after WP-1A bootstrap + WP-1C soft-miss fixes (#68) and FrameReader null-return fix (#69).** Command: `python3 tools/blob_walk_v2/make_walk_html_v2.py --walk --output-root blob_walk_v2/24corpus --intervals-from-corpus dump_step1/24corpus --workers 1`. Runtime: ~2.5 hours wall-clock (Lyra-Wheeling HEVC decode is the bottleneck; ~26 min FWD+BWD per interval pair on that video). Results: 24 interval cards, all 48 directions reach `stop_reason=hit_neighbor_seed` (0 gate_reject_cap, 0 miss_cap_no_blob). Aggregate FWD accepted=132, BWD accepted=49. After_walk_terminated=1 per direction (expected: the terminal neighbor-seed row). Step-1 rejected_motion_gate=6 across corpus (down from prior design where REJECT_CAP=1 halted all walkers at step 1). Key observations: Lyra-Wheeling [1131-1196] and [1196-1319] still show 0-1 accepted (all soft_miss_no_blob, zero residual signal); Lyra-Wheeling [15761-15834] FWD improved dramatically to 34 accepted (was halted at step 1 before); Conant [1389-1420] FWD=12 accepted; Lyra-Hersey [11600-11640] FWD=24 accepted.

### Additions and New Features

- **New test [test_frame_reader_no_null.py](../tests/test_frame_reader_no_null.py) enforces null-free FrameReader contract.** Two tests: `test_read_frame_eof_raises_runtime_error` (frame_index >= total_frames raises `RuntimeError`) and `test_read_frame_normal_returns_ndarray` (valid read returns `numpy.ndarray`, never `None`). Uses a `_FakeCapture` stub with `_apply_bin` overridden so no real video file is needed. Both pass in <0.1 s.
- **Updated [test_tr_residual_heat_map.py](../tests/test_tr_residual_heat_map.py) `test_compute_residual_bails_on_degenerate_roi`.** Changed assertion from `residual is None; mask is None` to `pytest.raises(ValueError, match="degenerate ROI")` to match new behavior where zero-area ROI raises ValueError instead of returning None.
- **Updated [test_tr_residual_motion_window.py](../tests/test_tr_residual_motion_window.py) `test_observe_blob_at_with_overrides_and_trace`.** Removed fragile `trace.roi_origin_xy is not None` assertion -- `roi_origin_xy` is only populated when raw blobs exist; on uniform gray (no motion) it remains None, which is correct. Replaced with `isinstance(trace.reject_reason, str)` to verify the trace is correctly marked with a string reject reason.

### Fixes and Maintenance

- **`read_frame()` in [frame_reader.py](../common_tools/frame_reader.py) no longer returns None; raises RuntimeError on EOF or unrecoverable decode failure.** Per FRAME_READER_NULL_AUDIT.md: (1) EOF guard added before seek -- if `frame_index >= total_frames` raises `RuntimeError` immediately; (2) Strategy-1 HEVC seek imprecision handled by retry: on `cap.read()` returning False, the code does `cap.set + cap.read` once more; if still False, raises `RuntimeError` with video path and frame index; (3) `__iter__` gets the same retry-then-raise logic instead of silently breaking iteration; (4) return type annotation changed from `numpy.ndarray | None` to `numpy.ndarray`. Root cause: HEVC-in-MKV iPhone source returns `(False, None)` on first read after non-keyframe seek even when the frame is decodable via sequential decode -- this was the dominant cause of 13 `residual_sum=0` corpus directions.
- **Downstream None guards deleted from [residual_motion.py](../track_runner/residual_motion.py) now that `read_frame()` never returns None.** Six guards removed: `_read_gray_frame` if-None return; `compute_residual_for_frame` center-frame if-None return; `compute_residual_for_frame` neighbor if-None continue (both the inner and the duplicate outer check); `_compute_residual_with_extras` center-frame if-None return; `_compute_residual_with_extras` neighbor if-None continue. Two sentinels changed to raises: `aligned_stack < 2` in production path now raises `RuntimeError("insufficient neighbors ...")` (was `return (None, None)`); same in extras path. `zero-ROI` guard changed from `return (None, None)` to `raise ValueError("degenerate ROI ...")` to surface the caller bug. `frame_n1` zero-substitution block replaced with an explicit EOF guard (`if frame_index + 1 < reader.frame_count`) so the extras path never silently returns zeros.
- **Downstream None guards deleted from [residual_pre_pass.py](../track_runner/residual_pre_pass.py).** Two guards removed: `if bgr is None: continue` in sequential walk loop; `if residual is None: continue` in `_compute_center`. Both are now dead code since `read_frame()` raises on failure and `compute_residual_for_frame` raises on insufficient neighbors.

### Decisions and Failures

- **24-corpus dump re-run after null-return fix: step_1 zero-residual count unchanged at 13 directions; reject_reason changed from `no_residual` to `acceptance_box_empty`.** All 13 previously null-returning directions now succeed through `read_frame()` and through `compute_residual_for_frame` (no RuntimeError raised on any of the 48 JSONs). However their `residual_sum` is still 0.0 -- the frames decode correctly but contain no temporal motion signal in the ROI. The `reject_reason` change from `no_residual` to `acceptance_box_empty` confirms the read path is working; the genuine zero-residual cause is a separate extraction/ROI problem (not a decode bug). Aggregate replay pass rate unchanged at 6/24 (25.0%) FAIL. Videos with 0/4 pass: IMG_3823, Jason-3200m-sectionals-IMG_4005, Lyra-Wheeling-IMG_3912. The HEVC seek-imprecision hypothesis from the audit is partially invalidated: the retry fix ensures frames decode, but the corpus zero-residual frames have no motion signal to extract regardless of decode strategy.

### Fixes and Maintenance

- **WP-1A bootstrap mode and WP-1C soft-miss walker landed in [walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py).** Two bugs fixed: (1) Bootstrap mode: steps 1 through BOOTSTRAP_N (N=1) now use `bootstrap_search_radius_w(fps) * torso_w` as the allowed displacement instead of the tracking-mode three-cap-min gate. At v_recent=0 and 60 fps the tracking gate allowed only 0.175W; the bootstrap gate correctly allows 0.80W (= 30/60 + 0.30). (2) Soft-miss walker: `REJECT_CAP`, `MISS_CAP`, and their `gate_reject_cap` / `miss_cap_no_blob` walk-stop branches are removed. Rejected-gate frames emit `status="rejected_motion_gate"` and the walker continues. No-blob frames emit `status="soft_miss_no_blob"` and the walker continues. Deterministic loop-bound guard (`step > abs(neighbor_frame - seed_frame) + 1`) added as the only overflow protection. Conant seed_3256 FWD walk verified: previously stopped at step 1 with `stop_reason=gate_reject_cap`, `fwd_accepted_count=1`; now reaches `stop_reason=hit_neighbor_seed` at frame 3272 with `fwd_accepted_count=2`. Zero `after_walk_terminated` rows on this interval. Unit tests in `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_bootstrap_radius.py` (8 tests, since removed) and `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_soft_miss.py` (7 tests, since removed) verified bootstrap gate, BOOTSTRAP_N=1, tracking gate divergence, and absence of REJECT_CAP/MISS_CAP constants.

### Behavior or Interface Changes

- **Inline SVG overlays replaced with PNG-baked overlays in `walk_html.py` and `walk_render.py`.** Per user decision: SVG too excessive. `walk_render.py` now bakes velocity vector (cv2.arrowedLine), allowed-jump circle (dashed cv2.ellipse), and residual line (green=accept, red=reject) directly into each per-frame PNG tile. Per-interval trajectory plots switched from inline SVG polylines to standalone PNG files rendered by matplotlib and written to `<seed_dir>/trajectory.png`. `walk_html.py` updated: all SVG emission code removed, `_compute_svg_overlay`/`_render_svg_overlay_html`/`_build_trajectory_svg` deleted, new `_render_trajectory_png` added, tile cells rendered with status-colored borders (green=accepted, red=rejected, orange=soft miss, etc.) matching v1 layout. `walk_svg_overlays` block in [overlay_styles.yaml](../track_runner/overlay_styles.yaml) renamed to `walk_overlays` (serves both render and HTML now). 24-corpus `walk.html` regenerated: 1845 lines (down from 3126), 0 `<svg>` tags (down from 246 inline SVG blocks), 21 trajectory PNGs written. Regenerate command: `source source_me.sh && python3 tools/blob_walk_v2/make_walk_html_v2.py --output-root blob_walk_v2/24corpus --intervals-from-corpus dump_step1/24corpus --skip-render`.

### Additions and New Features

- **`reject_reason` field added to per-step dicts in `dump_step1_inputs.py`.** Both step_0 and steps 1-3 now emit `reject_reason` from `trace.reject_reason` (a `BlobObserverTrace` field populated by `observe_blob_at` before each None-return). Value is the string reason (`no_residual`, `no_raw_blobs`, `corridor_empty`, `acceptance_box_empty`, `no_winner`) when observation failed, empty string when observation succeeded, `null` (None) when no trace was captured. Confirmed on `dump_step1/24corpus/IMG_3823/FWD_399.json` step_1: `reject_reason = 'acceptance_box_empty'` (residual_sum=0.0, obs_available=False). 48 JSONs regenerated; aggregate replay unchanged at `6/24 (25.0%) FAIL`.
- **`--intervals-from-corpus` flag added to `make_walk_html_v2.py`; 24-corpus `walk.html` generated at `walk.html`.** New `load_corpus_intervals(corpus_dir)` function reads per-video interval left_frame sets from `FWD_<left_frame>.json` filenames under a dump_step1 corpus directory. When `--intervals-from-corpus` is given, video discovery is replaced by corpus-directory enumeration and the walker is restricted to exactly those left_frames per video. Per-walk quality metrics added to each interval card in `walk.html` via three new fields in `walk_html.py`: `accepted_fraction` (accepted steps / total walk steps), `longest_no_accept_streak` (longest run of consecutive non-accepted frames), `final_displacement_to_neighbor_px` (Euclidean distance in px from last accepted position to neighbor seed). New `WalkQualityMetrics` dataclass and `_compute_walk_quality()` function added to `walk_html.py`. Run command: `source source_me.sh && python3 tools/blob_walk_v2/make_walk_html_v2.py --walk --output-root blob_walk_v2/24corpus --intervals-from-corpus dump_step1/24corpus --workers 1`. Output: 24 interval cards (4 per video x 6 videos), 246 SVG overlays, FWD+BWD quality metrics per card.

All 13 untestable directions have `residual_sum == 0` and correctly classify as `no_residual`. Fix: replaced the hard-coded string with `classify_failure_5bin(step1, left_seed, right_seed)` using the same `load_seed_pair` pattern already present in the testable branch. 24-corpus report regenerated: corrected histogram is `no_residual=13, no_raw_blobs=0, wrong_observable=11, true_gate_failure=3`; aggregate pass rate unchanged at `6/24 (25.0%) FAIL`.

### Additions and New Features

- **`--corpus-dir` flag added to `replay_step1.py`; 24-corpus REPLAY_REPORT.md generated at `REPLAY_REPORT.md`.** New `run_24corpus_report()` function generates a 7-section interval-level report: (a) header with corpus description, (b) aggregate verdict `6/24 (25.0%) -- FAIL`, (c) per-video table (6 videos x 4 intervals each), (d) FWD/BWD direction breakdown (48 total observations, 21/35 testable = 60.0%), (e) 5-bin failure histogram (no_raw_blobs=13, wrong_observable=11, true_gate_failure=3), (f) per-failed-interval table (18 failing intervals), (g) explicit numeric verdict. Both-non-testable intervals now count as failures (not skipped). Videos with 0/4 pass: IMG_3823, Jason-3200m-sectionals-IMG_4005, Lyra-Wheeling-IMG_3912. Dominant failure bin: `no_raw_blobs` (13 of 27 failing directions). `argparse` import added; `parse_args()` added. `--corpus-dir` path fully separate from original `dump_step1` report flow.

### Decisions and Failures

- **24-corpus M0 bar FAIL: interval-level pass rate 6/24 = 25.0%, below >50% bar.** Model 3 (3-frame sequence evaluate_model_3_sequence) tested on 24 well-formed intervals (4 random seed=42 per video, 6 outdoor 400m videos). An interval passes only when BOTH FWD and BWD sequences are testable and accepted. Result: Lyra-Hersey 3/4 (75%), Conant 2/4 (50%), IMG_3830 1/4 (25%), IMG_3823 0/4, Jason 0/4, Lyra-Wheeling 0/4. Total 6/24 = 25.0% FAIL. Dominant failure cause: `no_raw_blobs` (13 failing directions have empty corridor_blobs at step_1) -- extraction layer not producing usable blobs for a large fraction of corpus intervals.

### Behavior or Interface Changes

- **3-frame sequence Model 3 replaces single-frame bar; per-millisecond constants replace per-frame; capture schema extended to steps 0-3.** `walk_motion_gate.evaluate(...)` now requires explicit `source_fps` (no default), with `MAX_JUMP_W_PER_MS = 0.045` as the single source of truth for the per-frame budget (`0.045 * 1000 / source_fps`); duplicate per-model constant blocks in `replay_models.py` and `replay_step1.py` deleted -- Models 1, 2, 4, 5 now call `walk_motion_gate.evaluate` directly so replay never diverges from production; `walk_html.py` reads the constants from `walk_motion_gate`. `dump_step1_inputs.py` now captures step_0 plus step_1, step_2, step_3 (FWD steps 1..3 or BWD -1..-3) under a zero-velocity bootstrap prior and writes `source_fps` into each step dict. `replay_models.py` adds `evaluate_model_3_sequence` (per-step radius `i * radius_w_per_ms * (1000/fps) * torso_w`, all three steps must accept) and `is_testable_sequence`. `replay_step1.py` and `sweep_radius.py` drive the sequence; per-video bar stays at >51%. Recaptured 48 JSONs across the 6-video corpus. 3-frame pass rates at `radius_w_per_ms = 0.045` (= 0.75 W/frame at 60 fps): Conant 1/2, Jason 0/4, Lyra-Hersey 6/7, Lyra-Wheeling 0/3, IMG_3823 1/4, IMG_3830 5/8. Sweep over 0.030, 0.040, 0.045, 0.060, 0.080 W/ms finds NO universal-pass radius under the 3-frame bar; per-video winners: Conant 0.080, Lyra-Hersey 0.030, IMG_3830 0.040; Jason / IMG_3823 / Lyra-Wheeling have no passing radius. Reports regenerated: `REPLAY_REPORT.md`, `RADIUS_SWEEP.md`, `MODEL_DEFINITIONS.md`.

### Additions and New Features

- **`--random-corpus` flag added to `dump_step1_inputs.py`.** When set, ignores `--max-intervals` and picks 4 random well-formed intervals per video (seed pinned at 42, never re-rolled) from the `--videos` list, writing JSON files to `<output_dir>/24corpus/<video_basename>/`. Well-formed filter: both seeds post-race (`frame_index > race_start_frame`), neither flagged `not_in_frame`, both have derived geometry (`cx` present), interval length 2..10000 frames. New helper `is_well_formed_interval` and `select_random_corpus_intervals` implement the filter and selection. All step dicts (step_0, step_1, step_2, step_3) now include `residual_sum` (sum of pre-DoG residual pixel values in ROI) and `residual_nonzero_px` (count of non-zero pixels in ROI), allowing downstream classifiers to distinguish `no_residual` from `no_raw_blobs`. New helper `_residual_stats` computes both. 48 JSON files written to `dump_step1/24corpus/` covering 4 intervals per video across all 6 videos (Conant-4x400-2026_April_15, Jason-3200m-sectionals-IMG_4005, Lyra-Hersey-800m-IMG_3882, Lyra-Wheeling-IMG_3912, IMG_3823, IMG_3830); every video produced exactly 4 well-formed intervals.
- **5-bin failure taxonomy added to `replay_step1.py`.** New `classify_failure_5bin(replay_record, left_seed, right_seed)` classifies step-1 records into five user-pinned bins (`no_residual`, `no_raw_blobs`, `wrong_observable`, `candidate_selection`, `true_gate_failure`) plus `unclassified`. Decision tree: (1) `no_residual` when `residual_sum == 0` or `residual_nonzero_px == 0` (pre-task-53 captures without those fields fall back to `no_raw_blobs` with a warning); (2) `no_raw_blobs` when residual is non-zero but `obs_available` False or `corridor_blobs` empty; (3) `wrong_observable` when no corridor blob is within 0.5 * torso_w of interpolated runner center; (4) `candidate_selection` when runner blob exists in corridor but production winner is different or null; (5) `true_gate_failure` when winner IS the runner blob but motion gate rejected. Old classifier renamed `classify_failure_legacy` (backcompat). `IntervalClassification` gains `failure_bin_5`; report table gains `Failure Bin 5` column and 5-bin count table. 7 unit tests in `test_replay_step1_5bin.py`.
- **New all-in-one v2 driver `make_walk_html_v2.py`.** Reimplements the legacy v1 four-phase model (discover, walk, render, build walk.html) against the v2 stack (`walk_io`, `walk_driver.run_interval_walk`, `walk_render.render_walk_tile`, `walk_html.build_walk_html`). Adds `--outdoor-400m-only` corpus filter that reads `tr_config/<basename>.track_runner.config.yaml` and keeps only videos with `camera.track_size == 400`. CLI matches v1: `-o/--output-root`, `-w/--walk`, `-r/--resume`, `-j/--workers`, `-m/--max-videos`, `-s/--sort-by-size`, `-n/--sample-intervals-per-video`, `--skip-render`, `-d/--dry-run`, `-v/--videos`, `--outdoor-400m-only`.
- **M2-aligned SVG vector overlays in `walk_html.py`.** Each tile now carries an inline `<svg class="tile-svg">` overlay that draws (when CSV fields are populated): velocity vector with arrowhead marker, allowed-jump dashed circle, and residual line from prediction to candidate colored by gate decision (green=accept, red=reject). Each interval card also carries a per-interval `<svg class="trajectory">` polyline plot of accepted FWD/BWD walker positions plus filled seed markers, normalized to the bounding box of accepts plus seeds. Graceful degradation: when M1 schema fields (`vx_px`, `vy_px`, `max_displacement_px`) are absent the tile draws only the residual line and seed markers, and emits an HTML comment `<!-- M1 fields not yet present in CSV -->`. Palette block `walk_svg_overlays` added to [overlay_styles.yaml](../track_runner/overlay_styles.yaml); no hex colors hardcoded in `walk_html.py`.
