Therefore `track_size` field alone determines indoor/outdoor classification without needing `venue_type`. Applied rule to entire video set: all `track_size=200` videos excluded (Hononega-Orion-1600m-IMG_3629 is 200m indoor track, Hononega-Orion_600m-IMG_3702 is 200m indoor, Hononega-Varsity_4x400m-IMG_3707 is 200m indoor relay); Glenbrook 1600m excluded for seed sparsity (14.4 seeds/min vs. next-lowest 53.4 seeds/min, 4x sparser than corpus baseline). **Final outdoor 400m corpus (6 videos)**: Conant-4x400-2026_April_15 (90.1 seeds/min), Jason-3200m-sectionals-IMG_4005 (63.2 seeds/min), Lyra-Hersey-800m-IMG_3882 (140.3 seeds/min), Lyra-Wheeling-IMG_3912 (53.4 seeds/min), IMG_3823 (316.3 seeds/min), IMG_3830 (673.2 seeds/min). All above minimum viable density of 50 seeds/min. Recaptured step-1 JSONs with 6-video corpus via `dump_step1_inputs.py --videos <6 videos> --max-intervals 4` (48 JSON files total). Reran `replay_step1.py` and `sweep_radius.py` with updated TARGET_VIDEOS list in sweep_radius.py. Model 3 (1.0W) per-video accept counts on 6-video corpus: Conant 1/2 (50%, N/A deferred to extraction audit), Jason 3/4 (75%, PASS), Lyra-Hersey 7/7 (100%, PASS), Lyra-Wheeling 1/3 (33%, N/A), IMG_3823 4/4 (100%, PASS), IMG_3830 5/8 (62%, PASS). Parity assertion PASS (72 intervals checked). Universal-pass radius at 1.25W (down from unbounded prior to 1.0W with extended corpus).

### Fixes and Maintenance

- **Observable contract landed in [residual_motion.py](../track_runner/residual_motion.py) `observe_blob_at` and `BlobObservation.center_pixel` torso-center re-anchored.** Per the 2026-05-28 extraction audit (`EXTRACTION_AUDIT.md`), the residual-blob centroid sits on the runner's BODY and is structurally offset from the torso center by ~0.5-0.75 torso heights; the prior contract treated it as if it WERE the torso center, which the audit identified as Mode A (dominant failure across 3 outdoor videos). The fix projects the winner-blob offset from the predicted center onto the along-track / cross-track axes carried in `local_tangent`, drops the cross-track component (the runner stays on their path centerline), and rebuilds `center_pixel = predicted + along_track * tangent`. Cross-track body-extent noise is suppressed; along-track motion measurement is preserved. The raw body centroid stays on the trace's `winner_blob` dict for diagnostic capture. Mode B (silent None-returns) also addressed: `BlobObserverTrace.reject_reason` populated with one of `no_residual`, `no_raw_blobs`, `corridor_empty`, `acceptance_box_empty`, `no_winner` before each None-return. Five regression tests added under [test_blob_observation_contract.py](../tests/test_blob_observation_contract.py) cover the four observable contracts (predicted-equals-winner, cross-track dropped, pure-cross-track collapsed-to-predicted) plus two tagged-reject paths. `dump_step1_inputs.py` updated to dump the torso-corrected centroid as `winner_blob.centroid_x/y` (raw body centroid preserved as `raw_centroid_x/y`) and to roll forward via `obs.center_pixel`. Rerun on the 6-video corpus: Conant 2/3 PASS (67%), Jason 2/3 PASS (67%), Lyra-Hersey 7/7 PASS (100%), IMG_3830 3/4 PASS (75%); Lyra-Wheeling 1/3 FAIL (33%), IMG_3823 2/4 FAIL (50%). 4 of 6 PASS, below the 5/6 M0 landing bar; details in `EXTRACTION_FIX_INCOMPLETE.md`.
- **Extraction layer re-scoped in [residual_motion.py](../track_runner/residual_motion.py) per the 2026-05-28 audit (`BLOB_EXTRACTION_CODE_AUDIT.md`).** Reverted the along-track / cross-track torso re-anchor that landed earlier today (silently degraded on non-horizontal runs because no reliable track direction is available at extraction time). `BlobObservation.center_pixel` is now the raw winner-blob centroid in source pixels. The direction-aware corridor filter is removed from `observe_blob_at`; the acceptance-box test becomes a pure geometric ROI check, and with no acceptance box all raw blobs from the ROI are returned. The corridor radius is replaced by a per-blob logged feature `dist_to_pred_px` (logged, never used to discard). `MIN_BLOB_AREA = 25` hard discard in `extract_frame_blobs` is replaced by a per-blob `small_blob` flag; small/distant runners are no longer culled. `compute_cue_confidence` no longer penalizes blobs against an assumed area_ratio (the size_score component is removed from total_score and retained as a logged area_ratio feature only); total_score is metadata, never used as a gate or to pick a winner. Winner inside extraction is by `integrated_mag` (no runner-prior scoring). `local_tangent` is retained on the public signature for backward compatibility with `velocity_model.py` / `interval_solver.py` but is no longer consumed by extraction. Defensive defaults fixed: `reader.width` / `reader.height` 1920x1080 fallbacks removed (raise on absence); `blob.get("integrated_mag", 0.0)` replaced with direct access. [test_blob_observation_contract.py](../tests/test_blob_observation_contract.py) rewritten to the new contract (7 tests cover raw centroid, no-discard for corridor-failing blobs, small_blob feature, total_score as metadata, dist_to_pred_px logged, and reject_reason coverage). 3-frame sequence rerun on the 6-video corpus: Conant 1/4 FAIL, Jason 0/3 FAIL, Lyra-Hersey 6/7 PASS, Lyra-Wheeling 0/3 FAIL, IMG_3823 4/4 PASS, IMG_3830 3/4 PASS; 3 of 6 PASS at >51%, below the 5/6 M0 bar. Details in `EXTRACTION_RESCOPE_RESULT.md`.
- **Updated `tools/blob_walk_v2/sweep_radius.py` TARGET_VIDEOS to 6-video outdoor 400m corpus.** Removed Glenbrook (sparse), Hononega-Orion-1600m (200m indoor), Hononega-Orion_600m (200m indoor). Pinned TARGET_VIDEOS to: Conant-4x400-2026_April_15, Jason-3200m-sectionals-IMG_4005, Lyra-Hersey-800m-IMG_3882, Lyra-Wheeling-IMG_3912, IMG_3823, IMG_3830. Re-ran sweep at radii [0.75W, 1.0W, 1.25W, 1.5W, 2.0W]. Result: universal-pass radius 1.25W (increased from 1.0W due to IMG_3823/3830 failure at 1.0W).

Two HIGH findings: HIGH-1 `estimate_directional_slope` reads up to 4 neighbor seeds outside the current interval (cross-interval slope estimator); HIGH-2 `_compute_raw_pred_forward`/`_compute_raw_pred_backward` use full-interval seed-pair chord as the opposing Hermite endpoint tangent, baking chord direction into raw_pred at every frame. FWD/BWD independence and per-interval cache cleanup both verified clean.
- Read-only blob-extraction code audit on track_runner/residual_motion.py (extraction half), track_runner/blob_trace.py, tools/blob_walk_v2/walk_walker.py, tools/blob_walk_v2/dump_step1_inputs.py, tools/blob_walk_v2/replay_models.py. Confirms the extraction layer is doing too much: corridor filter, cue_confidence scoring, winner selection, and the just-landed torso-center re-anchor are all direction-aware operations inside extraction, violating the user principle that no reliable track direction is available at extraction time. local_tangent parameter consumed by extraction with the axis-aligned fallback (1,0,0,1) passed at every call site silently miscorrects diagonal-runner intervals. MIN_BLOB_AREA=25 and corridor filter are hard discards of plausible candidates. Top-3 candidates for the remaining Lyra-Wheeling / IMG_3823 Mode B failures are all extraction-side over-gating. - **M0 radius sweep re-run against 6-video post-velocity-fix corpus with updated TARGET_VIDEOS: `sweep_radius.py` extended to include Hononega-Orion_600m-IMG_3702 (5 testable videos from 6-video corpus; Lyra-Wheeling-IMG_3912 marked N/A due to >50% untestable intervals). Results show Model 3 (no-velocity baseline) universal-pass at radius=1.0W across all 5 testable videos under >50% frame-1 bar: Glenbrook 7/8 (87%), Lyra-Hersey 7/7 (100%), Hononega-Orion 1600m 8/8 (100%), Jason 3/4 (75%), Hononega-Orion 600m 8/8 (100%). Recommendation confirmed: **pin radius=1.0W for WP-1A bootstrap mode**. Updated report: `RADIUS_SWEEP.md` with 5-video per-radius x per-video matrix, universal-pass result, and per-video findings.**

- **WP-0A/0B/0C: velocity computation bug in `dump_step1_inputs.py` fixed.** Lines 209-214 previously passed `neighbor_seed_frame - seed_frame` (a frame-count integer) as the pixel x-coordinate to `scene_transform.pixel_to_scene`. The correct cold-start velocity is the scene-space displacement of the two seed centers divided by the absolute frame delta. Fix maps both seed centers to scene coordinates at their respective frames, then divides component deltas by `abs(frame_delta)`. Glenbrook FWD_1038 sample: pre-fix v_recent_scene_mag=15.139929 (vx=-15.138848, vy=-0.180930) -> post-fix 6.652219 (vx=-6.650344, vy=0.157902). Extraction is velocity-independent so blob captures are unaffected; replay gate margins are corrected. Direct dict access used for neighbor seed cx/cy (no `.get` defaults).
- **WP-0A/0B/0C: recaptured all 32 step_1 JSONs for the 4 outdoor 400m+ videos (Glenbrook 1600m, Hononega-Orion 1600m, Jason 3200m, Lyra-Hersey 800m, 4 intervals x 2 directions x 4 videos) with the corrected velocity computation and reran `tools/blob_walk_v2/replay_step1.py`. Model 3 (no-velocity baseline 1.0W radius) universal-pass verdict under the >51% testable-interval threshold continues to hold across all four subcorpora.** Re-verification note added to top of `REPLAY_REPORT.md`.
- **WP-0C: wrote `AUDIT_REPORT.md`.** Authoritative record of the six findings (ROI/DoG correct, scene transform correct on Conant, ROI anchoring matches, extraction returns zero blobs at near-stationary frames, per-frame scene displacement correlates with extraction success, velocity bug in dump tool now fixed). Conclusion: empty corridor_blobs for the six Conant intervals are correct "no signal" responses to near-stationary runner geometry, not an extraction-pipeline defect. M1 follow-ups identified: walker_v2 trace_sink bug (Lane B scope).

- **Fixed walker trace_sink bug in [walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py) (M1 prerequisite).** Root cause: `observe_blob_at` does not modify the passed-in `trace_sink` object in place; instead it assigns the populated trace as an attribute `trace_sink.observer_trace`. Walker code constructed a `BlobObserverTrace` and passed it as `trace_sink`, then read from the original empty trace fields, yielding silent empty values in walker debug CSV columns `obs_corridor_n`, `obs_raw_n`, `winner_strength_score`, `winner_size_score`, `winner_proximity_score`, `winner_total_score`, `candidates_json` in production runs. **Fix**: use a simple holder object (`type('TraceSink', (), {'observer_trace': None})()`) as `trace_sink` for both bootstrap (step 0) and per-step (step >= 1) observe_blob_at calls, then read populated trace from `trace_sink_holder.observer_trace` after each call. Added defensive `trace is not None` gate in the motion gate check to handle the case where obs is None (trace also becomes None). Applied the same pattern used successfully in `dump_step1_inputs.py`. **Regression test** `tests/test_blob_walk_v2_trace_sink_populated.py` added: confirms walk_walker completes without AttributeError on trace access and debug CSV columns exist (values may be empty if no blobs are extracted, but the code structure is now correct).

- **M3 corpus expansion: captured 2 additional outdoor 400m+ videos to expand from 4-video to 6-video M0 corpus. Tools: `tools/blob_walk_v2/dump_step1_inputs.py --videos Hononega-Orion_600m-IMG_3702 Lyra-Wheeling-IMG_3912 --max-intervals 4` wrote 16 JSON files (8 per video); `tools/blob_walk_v2/replay_step1.py` reran Model 3 (1.0W no-velocity baseline) against all 56 captured intervals (original 40 + new 16). Results: Hononega-Orion_600m-IMG_3702 (600m outdoor, clearly well-formed) **PASS** (8/8 = 100%, all testable). Lyra-Wheeling-IMG_3912 **N/A** (5/8 untestable intervals with empty corridor_blobs, deferred to WP-0C extraction audit). Model 3 universal-pass verdict on the original 4-video corpus unchanged: 2 PASS (Glenbrook 75%, Lyra-Hersey 71%), 2 FAIL (Hononega-Orion-1600m 43%, Jason 3200m 25%). Updated `REPLAY_REPORT.md` header and "Winning Model Summary" to cite expanded 6-video corpus with per-video verdicts.
