### Additions and New Features

- **M0 milestone synthesis written to `M0_SYNTHESIS.md`.** Long-form catch-up document covering the full M0 timeline (capture, replay, classification, threshold correction, expanded corpus, radius sweep, Conant audit, velocity bug, walker trace_sink bug), per-video interval and acceptance counts cited from `REPLAY_REPORT.md` and `RADIUS_SWEEP.md`, candidate-model shootout with per-video winners, four-bug summary (WP-0A trace_sink misuse, replay coordinate drift, walker_v2 trace_sink bug, velocity computation bug), Conant deferral rationale (near-stationary frames; correct pipeline behavior, no defect), threshold correction (>=7/8 -> >51%), M1 readiness (Model 3 at 1.0W pinned for bootstrap mode; Lane B vector-gate tracking unwritten; walker trace_sink fix landing as M1 prereq), open items (Conant deferred, box-level metrics excluded, identity-switch out of plan, M2 walk.html waiting on M1 schema close), and a reference index of every `dump_step1/` artifact. Documentation only; no production code, tests, or plan-file edits.

- **Short interval residual extraction analysis: soft_miss_no_blob is correct behavior.** Side quest investigation of 6 near-stationary Conant intervals with empty step_1 corridor_blobs (per WP-0C finding). Analyzed 40 intervals across 5 videos (Conant 4x400, Glenbrook 1600m, Hononega-Orion 1600m, Jason 3200m, Lyra-Hersey 800m). Computed per-interval metrics: frame_delta, per-frame scene displacement (recent motion / frames), step_0 and step_1 blob presence. **Result:** No clean threshold separates extraction success from failure. Failures occur across all displacement ranges (0.027 to 5.0 scene px/frame) and interval lengths (8 to 102 frames). Direction sensitivity observed: identical seed geometry sometimes succeeds FWD but fails BWD. Failures show mixed step_0 blob presence (some have step_0 blob, some don't). **Conclusion:** Soft miss is expected behavior when residual-motion signal is weak, noisy, or image-based (saturation, occlusion, lighting). Not a design bug. Recommendation: Accept soft misses; if quality improvement needed, investigate frame-by-frame image characteristics, ROI positioning, and corridor gate tuning rather than pre-filtering by motion or interval length. Output: `SHORT_INTERVAL_REPORT.md` with per-interval table, displacement vs. success scatter, and analysis.

- **WP-0B bar-threshold correction: strict majority replaces 7/8.** User correction 2026-05-27: the prior `FRAME_1_ACCEPTANCE_BAR_FRACTION = 7.0 / 8.0` (87.5%) was too high. Updated `tools/blob_walk_v2/replay_step1.py` to `FRAME_1_ACCEPTANCE_BAR_FRACTION = 0.51` (strict majority) and changed the per-subcorpus PASS test from `accepts >= ceil(0.51 * testable)` to `accepts > 0.51 * testable` (strict greater-than). Regenerated `dump_step1/REPLAY_REPORT.md`: Hononega-Orion 1600m (4/7 = 57%) and Jason 3200m (3/4 = 75%) flip from FAIL to PASS, joining Glenbrook 1600m (7/8) and Lyra-Hersey 800m (7/7). **Overall verdict: PASS across all 4 outdoor 400m+ videos with Model 3 (no-velocity 1.0W radius)**; Conant remains N/A and deferred to WP-0C.

- **WP-0B radius sweep diagnostic tool and universal-pass verdict.** New tool `tools/blob_walk_v2/sweep_radius.py` replays Model 3 (no-velocity baseline) at 5 candidate radii (0.75W, 1.0W, 1.25W, 1.5W, 2.0W) across all captured JSONs from 4 outdoor 400m+ videos. For each radius, accepts if any corridor blob centroid (scene coords) falls within `radius * torso_w_px` of `prev_scene`, skips untestable intervals (no corridor blobs), and computes per-video acceptance fraction. Applies the >50% (simple majority) frame-1 bar. **Result: Model 3 at 1.0W is the smallest universal-pass radius**, passing all 4 videos (Glenbrook 7/8, Lyra-Hersey 7/7, Hononega-Orion 4/7, Jason 3/4). Earlier 1.0W-only baseline failed Hononega-Orion and Jason at the strict 7/8 bar; looser >50% bar confirms that 1.0W width radius (the current production default) is now sufficient for all 4 targets once intervals with empty corridor_blobs are excluded. Recommendation: **pin radius=1.0W for WP-1A bootstrap mode**. Smaller radii (0.75W) fail 2-3 videos; larger radii (1.25-2.0W) pass all videos but increase false positives and are not necessary. Output: `dump_step1/RADIUS_SWEEP.md` with per-radius x per-video matrix, failing-video triage, and recommendation.

- **WP-0B split-verdict reframe: per-subcorpus bar computation recognizes Glenbrook PASS, defers Conant to WP-0C.** The prior bar verdict incorrectly applied the 7/8 (87.5%) threshold across all 16 intervals (8 Glenbrook + 8 Conant), requiring 14+ accepts globally. However, 6 of 8 Conant intervals have empty `corridor_blobs` and are not testable under any motion model. **Root cause reframe**: Testability is per-subcorpus. Glenbrook has 8 testable intervals (all have non-zero allowed_jump_scene); Conant has only 2 testable intervals (6 have 0.0 allowed and 0.0 actual margins, indicating no corridor blob to evaluate). **Fix applied to `tools/blob_walk_v2/replay_step1.py`**: Modified bar-verdict computation to (1) group results by video basename, (2) for each subcorpus, count testable intervals (those with `allowed_jump_scene > 0`), (3) apply the 7/8 bar threshold strictly to testable intervals per subcorpus, (4) mark a subcorpus N/A if > 50% of its total intervals are untestable. **New verdict**: Glenbrook PASS with Model 3 (no-velocity baseline 1.0W radius) at 7/8 testable intervals (87.5%); Conant N/A-deferred-to-WP-0C (6 of 8 untestable). Overall verdict: **PARTIAL PASS** (Glenbrook passes; Conant audit scope deferred to WP-0C). Regenerated REPLAY_REPORT.md with "M0 Win" section and per-subcorpus bar verdict table. Next step: dispatch WP-1A against Model 3 as bootstrap-mode winning candidate; WP-0C investigates Conant empty corridors in parallel.

- **WP-0B corpus expansion: captured 3 additional outdoor 400m+ track events (Hononega-Orion 1600m, Jason 3200m, Lyra-Hersey 800m, 4 intervals per video x 2 directions = 24 new JSONs) and reran replay shootout across all 4 outdoor videos (32 testable intervals total; Glenbrook 1600m captured earlier). Per-video bar verdicts (strict >=7/8 threshold):
  - 2025-Glenbrook_South-1600m-IMG_1503: **PASS** (7/8 = 87.5%)
  - Hononega-Orion-1600m-IMG_3629: **FAIL** (4/7 testable = 57%)
  - Jason-3200m-sectionals-IMG_4005: **FAIL** (3/4 testable = 75%)
  - Lyra-Hersey-800m-IMG_3882: **PASS** (7/7 testable = 100%)
  - **Overall: 2 PASS, 2 FAIL across 4 outdoor 400m+ videos.** Model 3 (no-velocity baseline 1.0W radius) dominates Glenbrook and Lyra-Hersey decisively. Replicability confirmed on two independent 1600m+ videos, but multi-video evidence shows the model is not uniformly strong: Hononega-Orion and Jason both show dominant failure mode "three_cap_min_too_strict" (gate rejects valid blobs on mid-race intervals), suggesting the gate's rigid 1.0W radius is too conservative for wider motion envelopes. Dominant failure mode across 40 captured intervals: "other" (17/40, non-motion-related issues). Conant 4x400 remains excluded per task scope (indoor short relay, not outdoor 400m+). Updated `REPLAY_REPORT.md` with expanded corpus analysis.

- **WP-0B frame-1 acceptance bar: strict threshold applied and outlier triage completed.** Prior REPLAY_REPORT.md shipped a "PASS: Model 3" verdict that violated the plan's strict bar: Model 3 accepted on 8 of 16 intervals (50%), but the threshold is >= 7 of 8 (87.5% = 14 of 16). **Fix**: Added named constant `FRAME_1_ACCEPTANCE_BAR_FRACTION = 7.0 / 8.0` to `tools/blob_walk_v2/replay_step1.py` with clear citation of plan section M0 WP-0B. Bar-verdict computation now computes `min_accepts_required = ceil(FRAME_1_ACCEPTANCE_BAR_FRACTION * total_intervals)` and checks `accepts_count >= min_accepts_required`. With 16 captured intervals, the bar requires 14+ accepts; all six candidate models fail on this strict metric, generating verdict = **FAIL**. **Outlier triage**: Added section "Outlier Triage" to REPLAY_REPORT.md. (1) Glenbrook FWD 1470-1680 margin -2552.1: captured JSON shows `obs_available=True`, `corridor_blobs=1`, `actual_jump=2571.14 scene px`, `allowed_jump=14.25 scene px`. This is a valid rejection of genuine large inter-frame motion over a 210-frame interval, not a coordinate parity bug. Reclassified to **valid_reject_runner_actually_moved_too_much**. (2) Six Conant zero-margin intervals (BWD 1080-1111, BWD 1126-1134, FWD 1080-1111, FWD 1111-1126, FWD 1126-1134, FWD 1134-1142): all have `corridor_blobs=[]` and `obs_available=False`, indicating no blobs were extracted. All models trivially reject with 0.0 margin (no candidate to evaluate). Reclassified to **extraction_or_transform_bug**, pointing to WP-0C (extraction/transform audit) if the bar fails. Report now includes threshold statement and per-interval classification updates. Next step: dispatch WP-0C per plan gate condition (bar fails -> extraction audit; bar passes after triage -> proceed to M1 WP-1A).

- **WP-0B coordinate parity fix: replay models now convert blob centroids from source-frame pixels to scene coordinates.** The captured JSON stores blob centroids in source-frame pixel coordinates (from `BlobObserverTrace`), but the replay models were comparing them directly to scene-space coordinates without conversion. This caused spurious large actual_jump values (4000-8000 scene pixels) during replay, while the live `walk_motion_gate.evaluate()` correctly computed small jumps. **Fix**: Added coordinate conversion in `replay_step1.py` via `scene_transform.pixel_to_scene(frame_index, blob_cx_px, blob_cy_px)` before passing blob centroids to all 6 motion models. **Parity assertion**: Model 1 (current scalar three-cap-min gate) was verified to match the live gate result within 0.5 scene pixels margin on all 16 captured intervals. All parity checks PASSED, confirming the fix resolves the diagnostic mismatch.

- **Fixed WP-0A and WP-0B critical trace_sink bug in `tools/blob_walk_v2/dump_step1_inputs.py` and updated `tools/blob_walk_v2/replay_step1.py` with Models 2 and 4.** Root cause M0 finding: `observe_blob_at` does NOT modify the passed-in `trace_sink` object in place; instead it assigns the populated trace as an attribute `trace_sink.observer_trace`. WP-0A code (and production walker) constructed a `BlobObserverTrace` and passed it as `trace_sink`, then read from the original empty trace fields, yielding empty `corridor_blobs`, `winner_blob`, and `raw_blobs` in all 16 captured JSONs. **Fix**: use a simple holder object (`type('Holder', (), {'observer_trace': None})()`) as `trace_sink`, then read populated fields from `trace_sink.observer_trace` after the call. Applied to both step-0 and step-1 capture in `dump_step1_inputs.py`. **New capture**: re-run with fixed code. Expected: at least 10 of 16 step_1 JSONs now have non-empty `corridor_blobs` (matching the `obs_available=True` count). **WP-0B update**: Added Models 2 and 4 to the candidate shootout. Model 2: Revised vector-prediction three-cap-min gate (uses `(vx_scene, vy_scene)` vector, predicts `pred_scene = prev_scene + v_scene * dt`, evaluates residual against three-cap-min gate). Model 4: Seed-chord short-lookahead prior (scales velocity by lookahead factor, cold-start from 10-frame lookahead of the seed chord, uses three-cap-min gate). All 6 models now tagged `uses_endpoint_seeds=True` and `uses_intermediate_labels=False` (allowed live-gate inputs). Shootout matrix expanded from 4 to 6 columns in REPLAY_REPORT.md. Re-run replay against fixed JSONs; regenerate REPLAY_REPORT.md with real corridor blobs and refreshed frame-1 acceptance bar verdict. **WP-0C finding (conditional)**: The production walker at `tools/blob_walk_v2/walk_walker.py` has the same `trace_sink` bug. File constructs `BlobObserverTrace` and passes as `trace_sink`, then reads outer trace fields, so walker CSV columns `obs_corridor_n`, `obs_raw_n`, `winner_strength_score`, `winner_size_score`, `winner_proximity_score`, `winner_total_score`, `candidates_json` are silently empty in the walker debug CSVs. Documented in REPLAY_REPORT.md WP-0C section for M1 fix (M0 does not modify production walker per scope).

- **New tool `dump_step1_inputs.py`.** Step-0 and step-1 input dumper for blob walker v2 (WP-0A falsification step). Captures raw walker inputs to JSON for later analysis by WP-0B. For each FWD/BWD direction on post_start intervals, emits a JSON file with: seed torso geometry, predicted center, previous accepted position, cold-start velocity (scene coords), all corridor blobs with per-blob fields (centroid, area, integrated_mag, in_corridor, in_acceptance_box, dist_to_pred_px, scoring terms), production winner blob, motion gate result (all fields: accepted, expected_jump, allowed_jump, actual_jump, v_recent_scene_mag, dt_for_gate, reject_reason), ROI origin, and acceptance box bounds. Output: `dump_step1/<video_basename>/<direction>_<seed_frame>.json`. Executable with shebang; flags: `-v/--videos` (space-separated basenames), `-i/--max-intervals N` (cap post_start per video), `-o/--output-dir` (default `dump_step1`). Smoke tested on 2 videos with 8+ intervals each; all 16 FWD/BWD JSONs written without exception, all fields present in output. No production module edits; reuses walk_io, walk_motion_gate, residual_motion, blob_trace. **Quality audit of WP-0A capture step:** (1) Field rename: `prev_scene_x` and `prev_scene_y` replaced with `prev_cx_scene` and `prev_cy_scene` (spec compliance per plan WP-0A). (2) Removed defensive `.get()` defaults on blob extraction (centroid_x, centroid_y, area, integrated_mag, in_corridor, in_acceptance_box, dist_to_pred_px, strength_score, size_score, proximity_score, total_score) per `docs/PYTHON_STYLE.md` "DO NOT HIDE BUGS WITH DEFAULTS"; direct dict access now allows loud failure on missing required keys instead of silent None. (3) Removed broad `try/except Exception` blocks in `main()` (per `docs/PYTHON_STYLE.md` avoid try/except); errors propagate immediately instead of silent continue, improving debuggability. (4) Added `audit_winner_alternatives` field (list of 3 entries for center_of_mass, strongest_blob, body_position rules via `walk_walker.resolve_audit_winner()`) to both step 0 and step 1 output per plan WP-0A spec "Selected production winner and any audit-winner alternatives." Capture rerun: 16 JSON files (2 videos x 4 intervals x 2 directions) written to `dump_step1/` with all fixes verified.

## 2026-05-25

### Fixes and Maintenance

- **Mode docs refreshed from the live parser.** [TARGET.md](modes/TARGET.md) now documents the new target shortcuts; [SOLVE.md](modes/SOLVE.md) and [REFINE.md](modes/REFINE.md) were refreshed by `tools/refresh_mode_docs.py` to match existing `--auto-bin` parser help.
- **`tools/blob_walk_v2/walk_walker.py` dedups `after_walk_terminated` diagnostic rows against frames already written by the main walk loop (tracked via `visited_frames` set), eliminating duplicate frame_index rows in walker CSV output.**
- **Walker acceptance box made vertically symmetric (+/-0.75h) in [walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py).** Bootstrap and per-step boxes now extend equally above and below the anchor (total 1.5h) so head and thigh motion blobs are caught equally; x range unchanged (+/-0.5w), motion gate constants unchanged.
- **Walker per-step geometry fix: use seed-local body acceptance box at every step to catch thigh-resident motion blobs.** At step >= 1, walker now constructs an acceptance box anchored to the walker's prior position (prev_cx/cy from last accept + velocity projection) instead of the seed center. Box shape is tight x (+/-0.5 torso_w) and extends 1.5h downward to include thigh motion residual. This replaces the default corridor radius which was too narrow vertically to catch motion peaks at the runner's lower torso. Motion gate constants unchanged; improvement is purely in observer search geometry. Fixes Glenbrook 16590-16800 early termination at step 1 where thigh blob (0.80 W below seed center) exceeded the 0.75 W per-step cap only because it fell outside the vertical corridor; now the body box catches it within tolerance.
- **interval_summary.csv field rename and new fields for walker progress visibility.** Renamed `fwd_lock_length` -> `fwd_interval_length` and `bwd_lock_length` -> `bwd_interval_length` (full bracketed distance, independent of walker outcome). Added four new columns: `fwd_accepted_count` (frames walker accepted on FWD pass), `bwd_accepted_count` (same for BWD), `fwd_last_accepted_offset` (distance in frames from FWD seed to last accepted frame), `bwd_last_accepted_offset` (same for BWD). These fields allow at-a-glance diagnosis of early termination: a 16590-frame interval with fwd_interval_length=210 but fwd_accepted_count=1 and fwd_last_accepted_offset=0 shows walker died at step 1.
- **Fixed walk_walker.py observer invariant violation: when obs is None, discard trace and emit soft_miss_no_blob.** When `observe_blob_at` returns None (empty corridor), the previous code still emitted stale trace data: candidates_json populated from an empty trace, obs_corridor_n logged as 0, but cand_cx/cand_cy and motion-gate fields also populated, creating internally inconsistent CSV rows. The fix ensures: (1) if obs is None, trace object is discarded immediately, (2) per-step candidates_json and obs_* fields are only written when obs is not None, (3) the none branch sets status to soft_miss_no_blob and leaves cand_cx/cand_cy/candidates_json/obs_corridor_n blank, (4) module-level docstring documents unit conventions (scene vs. pixel coordinates) for future clarity on the known motion-gate inconsistency. Satisfies required invariant 1 from the spec: single source of truth for observation; when obs is None, status must be soft_miss_no_blob and candidate fields must be blank.
- **Four UX fixes to blob_walk_v2 output: legend position, caption content, interval spread, pred projection.** (1) Legend moved to top of walk.html immediately after page title; now includes color key for walker_overlays (seven semantic roles: winner yellow, corridor non-winner cyan, raw outside corridor gray, rejected winner light red, prior cross magenta, acceptance box yellow-dashed, audit disagreement white square) and per-tile caption field reference. (2) Tile captions replace "(no data)" with frame_index, status, stop_reason (always shown) plus optional dt, jump W-fractions, obs_confidence, winner_mode, audit_rule; ensures every tile carries identifying information for user triage. (3) Driver interval selection changed from `[:max_intervals]` (first-N) to `_evenly_spread` (first/middle/last distributed), so `-i 3` on an 11-interval corpus picks indices [0, 5, 10] instead of [0, 1, 2]. (4) After_walk_terminated diagnostic rows now project pred_cx/cy via linear interpolation between bracketing seed-pair positions, so magenta + tracks the estimated runner position as walker steps advance; fallback to last_accepted or seed_center if neighbor_seed coordinates unavailable. walk_walker.walk_one_direction gained optional `neighbor_seed_cx`, `neighbor_seed_cy` kwargs; walk_driver passes them from the bracketing seed dict on both FWD and BWD calls. ABSOLUTE_MAX_JUMP_W (1.5) added to HTML legend constants. Fixes user observations: legend discovery, caption ambiguity, interval clustering, and prior position drift.

### Removals and Deprecations

- **Archived [blob_walker_velocity_gate_spec_request.md](archive/blob_walker_velocity_gate_spec_request.md) to `docs/archive/`.** Spec failed 3-frame replay validation 2026-05-28 (4 of 6 videos failed frame-1 acceptance bar); replaced by [windowed_path_selection_amendment.md](archive/windowed_path_selection_amendment.md). A `## SUPERSEDED 2026-05-28` block was prepended at the top of the archived spec pointing readers at the replacement.
- **Removed unauthorized `past_stop` render state from blob_walk.** The walker, the render pass, and `walk.html` no longer invent a "past stop" pseudo-status for sampled offsets the walker did not visit. Contract C4 names pre-race as the only special-case regime; there is no approved post-stop state. `tools/blob_walk/render_full_walk.py` drops the FWD/BWD past-stop rendering blocks and the `past_stop` entry from `STATUS_BORDER_COLOR`; `tools/blob_walk/walk_html.py` drops `past_stop` from `STATUS_COLORS` / `STATUS_SHORT_LABEL` / legend and `_render_column_cells` skips offsets the walker did not reach. 140 orphan past-stop PNGs removed from `blob_walk/visual/`.

### Additions and New Features

- **New tool `walk_driver.py`.** Corpus-scale driver for blob walker v2: executable with shebang, orchestrates FWD/BWD walks on seed-to-seed intervals from a video corpus, renders tiles, writes CSVs, and builds HTML. Exports `parse_args()` returning argparse.Namespace with flags: `-v/--videos` (space-separated video basenames), `-i/--max-intervals N` (cap post_start intervals per video, default all), `-o/--output-dir` (output root, default `blob_walk_v2/`), `-r/--resume` (skip intervals with CSV + at least one tile), `--no-render` (skip PNG rendering), `--no-html` (skip HTML build), `-w/--winner-mode {production_winner,audit_winner}` (default production_winner), `--audit-rule {center_of_mass,strongest_blob,body_position}` (required for audit_winner). Sequential single loop: loads seeds, reader, scene transform per video; enumerates intervals via `walk_io.enumerate_seed_to_seed_intervals()`; drops pre_start intervals silently, surfaces crossing_race_start in log with skip_reason; for each post_start interval runs `walk_walker.walk_one_direction()` twice (FWD with sign=+1, BWD with sign=-1), both sharing winner_mode; renders tiles per accepted frame via `walk_render.render_walk_tile()` (catches RuntimeError per-interval, records render_error flag, continues); writes `interval_summary.csv` with walker_run, skip_reason, lock lengths, stop reasons, gap, render_error, mode_disagreement_count; at end calls `walk_html.build_walk_html()` if not --no-html. Exception handling is per-interval: walker and renderer raise loudly, driver catches and continues. Output layout: `{output_dir}/{video_basename}/seed_{F_L}_{F_R}/{fwd_verdicts.csv, bwd_verdicts.csv, interval_summary.csv, fwd/frame_N.png, bwd/frame_N.png}` with `walk.html` at root. Smoke tested with real corpus video (2 intervals, --no-html) and resume path (--no-render --resume). Implements WP-3C of the active plan.
- **New module `walk_html.py`.** Diagnostic HTML builder for blob walker v2 output. Function `build_walk_html(run_root: pathlib.Path) -> pathlib.Path` walks the output directory and writes `<run_root>/walk.html`. UX mirrors v1: dark monospace, per-interval cards, FWD/BWD twin grids, status borders (neutral gray), click-to-enlarge PNG links (target="_blank"), legend in HTML only. Tile sampling: power-of-2 offsets {0, 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, ...} up to interval_length PLUS dense-fill offsets in failure windows (between walker stop_row and next power-of-2 boundary, enabling frame-by-frame step through failure transition). Every sampled offset produces a tile; per-tile caption exposes: dt (frames since last accept), actual_jump / allowed_jump (W-fractions, two decimals), obs_confidence (two decimals), winner_mode, audit rule name on mode disagreement. Per-interval summary line: FWD walk-interval length, BWD walk-interval length, gap, stop reasons (FWD and BWD), max actual_jump as W-fraction, median actual_jump as W-fraction, mode-disagreement count. Legend includes gate constants verbatim (MAX_JUMP_W = 0.75, VELOCITY_TOLERANCE = 1.75, DT_GATE_CAP = 3, MEASUREMENT_ALLOWANCE_W = 0.10) and the real-world sanity comment from walk_motion_gate.py rendered as <pre> block. Missing PNG aborts the build LOUDLY with RuntimeError (driver WP-3C catches per interval; HTML builder does not silently degrade). Hand-written HTML string (no Jinja2), ASCII-only output, tabs in source, type hints, no `dict.get` defaults for required CSV columns, no try/except for control flow. Implements WP-3B of the active plan.
- **New module [walk_motion_gate.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_motion_gate.py).** Motion gate for blob walker v2: per-frame jump acceptance gate. Pure math (no I/O). Evaluates whether a candidate blob position is physically plausible given recent velocity. Uses torso WIDTH as the motion scale unit (not height) per contract C2. Exports `MotionGateResult` dataclass and `evaluate()` function accepting scene-space position, velocity, frame count, torso width, and radial drift fraction. Gate formula includes velocity-driven allowance, centroid-noise allowance, per-frame cap scaled by dt_for_gate, and absolute hard ceiling. Reject reasons: "absolute_cap", "per_step_cap", "velocity_tolerance". Smoke tested with 11 unit tests covering cold-start, centroid jitter, all three cap modes, dt clamping, radial allowance, and per-step scaling. Implements WP-2B of the active plan.
- **New module `walk_io.py`.** I/O glue layer for the v2 walker. Pure orchestration with no compute. Four functions: `load_walker_seeds(video_basename)` returns the `state_io.load_seeds` dict with derived cx/cy/w/h; `open_walker_reader(video_basename)` returns `(FrameReader, probe_info)` composed via `probe_video` and `FrameReader`; `load_walker_scene_transform(video_basename)` returns a `SceneTransform` built from `camera_motion.load_motion_cache`; `enumerate_seed_to_seed_intervals(seeds_dict, race_start_frame)` returns a list of SeedToSeedInterval dicts with left_seed, right_seed, and label (post_start, crossing_race_start, or pre_start). Implements WP-2A of the active plan.
- **New module [walk_debug_log.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_debug_log.py).** Per-frame walker telemetry CSV writer with locked header (39 columns). Exports `DebugLogRow` dataclass, `DebugLogWriter` class with context-manager support, and `HEADER` tuple. Unavailable numeric values render as blank cells (empty strings), never the literal string "None". Status enum (accept-list): accepted, soft_miss_no_blob, soft_miss_low_conf, rejected_motion_gate, after_walk_terminated. `candidates_json` cell carries JSON-serialized list of every blob from `trace.raw_blobs &cup; trace.corridor_blobs` with per-blob fields: centroid_x/y, area, integrated_mag, in_acceptance_box, in_corridor, dist_to_pred_px, strength/size/proximity/total_score. Smoke tested: write-read roundtrip, header match, no literal "None" in output, blank cell semantics, and row count. Implements WP-2D of the active plan.
- **New module [walk_walker.py](../track_runner/blob_walk/walk_walker.py).** One-direction blob walker: frame-by-frame step from seed toward neighbor seed. Exports `WalkSummary` dataclass with accepts, stop_frame, stop_reason, total_frames_visited, mode_disagreement_count. Function `walk_one_direction()` implements bootstrap seed-local shape observe_blob_at call (tight ROI/DoG/acceptance_box per WP-2C spec), per-step production-shape calls, motion gating via `walk_motion_gate.evaluate()`, and termination on natural conditions (no artificial step ceiling). Supports two winner modes: "production_winner" (default, use trace.winner_blob) and "audit_winner" (diagnostic, resolve via one of three rules: center_of_mass, strongest_blob, body_position). Helper `resolve_audit_winner()` pure function; walker itself has no Hermite imports (enforced by AST test). Cold-start velocity seeded from seed-chord distance; post-step velocity from rolling window of recent accepts. Smoke tested with 5 unit tests on winner modes and dataclass fields. Implements WP-2C of the active plan.
- **New helper `compute_heat_map_overlay_roi` in [residual_heat_map.py](../track_runner/residual_heat_map.py).** Sibling to `compute_heat_map_roi` that returns transparent BGRA heat-map overlays instead of opaque BGR composites. Alpha channel is 0 (fully transparent) below threshold and `int(blend_alpha * 255)` above threshold, enabling downstream alpha-blending at the requested opacity. Reuses residual computation, DoG band-pass, and JET colorization to avoid reimvention in tools like `blob_walk`. Private helper `_build_jet_layer` extracted for code reuse; `compute_heat_map_roi` remains byte-identical. New smoke test in [test_tr_residual_heat_map.py](../tests/test_tr_residual_heat_map.py) verifies BGRA output shape and alpha channel semantics.
- **New tool `make_walk_html.py`.** All-in-one driver for the blob walk pipeline. Auto-discovers videos from `tr_config/*.track_runner.seeds.json` + matching `TRACK_VIDEOS/<basename>.mkv`. Four phases: (0) discover + sort + select; (1) walker batch via `batch.run_batch` (`--walk`); (2) render walker tiles (`render_full_walk.render_all`, supports `--workers N` parallel-over-videos and `--resume` skip existing PNGs); (3) build `walk.html` (`walk_html.build_walk_html`). Flags: `--walk`, `--resume`, `--workers N`, `--max-videos N` (alias `--limit`), `--sort-by-size` (ascending by `width*height*fps*frame_count`), `--sample-intervals-per-video N`, `--skip-render`, `--dry-run`. `batch.run_batch` and `render_full_walk.render_all` gained `max_intervals_per_video` and `videos`/`resume`/`workers` parameters respectively to support the driver.
- **New doc [TR_DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md](TR_DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md).** Names the existing track-runner primitives that new tools should route through (seed loading via `state_io.load_seeds`, scene transform via `camera_motion.load_motion_cache`, ROI heat-map via `residual_heat_map.compute_heat_map_roi`, blob extraction via `residual_motion.observe_blob_at`, video frame reads via `common_tools.frame_reader.FrameReader`). Each section contrasts a good code pattern with a bad one and names the reinvention trap. Written after a session that reintroduced top-left-as-center, ad-hoc residual+DoG+extract chains, and persistent `SceneTransform.pkl` in five different blob_walk files. Frame-reader section added 2026-05-25 after audit confirmed every blob_walk module routes reads through `config_io.open_reader` (no cv2.VideoCapture / imageio reinventions).
- **New tool `render_report.py`.** Computes per-variant, per-video, and corpus-wide verdict tables for the blob_walk experiment from `per_variant_summary.csv` + every `fwd_bwd_convergence.csv` under the run root. Applies the plan's INSUFFICIENT_OVERLAP cap (PROMISING ceiling when > 30 percent of intervals fail the overlap gate). Includes a bug-impact section that re-runs the same denominator math against the archived pre-fix run so deltas land on a like-for-like axis. Decision-tree routing block at the bottom selects the best variant by absolute convergent count.
- **New tool `sanity_pack.py`.** Renders per-seed inspection PNGs comparing default `observe_blob_at` output to the seed-local override extraction. Each PNG draws the seed torso box, the override acceptance box (cyan dashed), the default-observer winner (magenta cross), and the override winner (lime cross) over a transparent heat-map overlay. Source crop ~3h around the seed center. Used to triage whether failed walks are tracking errors, bad seeds, or rendering/coordinate bugs.
- **New artifact `INTEGRATION_ASSESSMENT.md`.** Three-path production-integration assessment for the blob walker (Plan A Stage 4b seed verifier, Plan B gate-ablation experiment, Plan C Stage 4 replacement). Recommends Plan A first: lands the per-seed acceptance-box quality flag into production, consumed by Stage 3 / 4 score weighting and the UI seed-review tool. No contract amendment needed for Plan A. Plan B is the prerequisite for any Stage 4 replacement and runs as a side-quest experiment. Plan B result added in-place 2026-05-25 after the gate-ablation batch landed.
- **New artifact `ABLATION_REPORT.md`.** Plan B gate-ablation report comparing four-variant matrix `{motion-term in scoring} x {direction-reversal hard-stop}`. Output of two new variants V_NO_DIR_REVERSAL_RAW (motion-term ON, direction-reversal OFF) and V_NO_MOTION_TERM_RAW (motion-term OFF, direction-reversal ON) added to `tools/blob_walk/variants.py` under `ABLATION_VARIANTS`. Renderer `tools/blob_walk/render_ablation_report.py` computes the matrix from the existing `per_variant_summary.csv` plus the per-variant convergence CSVs.
- **New tool `render_ablation_report.py`.** Renders the four-cell `{motion-term} x {direction-reversal hard-stop}` gate-state matrix from the per-variant blob_walk artifacts. Imports the shared aggregator from `tools/blob_walk/render_report.py` (`_classify_verdict`, `_load_convergence_median_per_interval`, `_load_summary_rows`) so verdict math is shared with the primary report. Output: `blob_walk/ABLATION_REPORT.md`.
- **New module `walk_render.py`.** Per-frame PNG renderer for blob walker v2 tiles. Function `render_walk_tile` reads source frame, computes transparent JET heat-map overlay via `residual_heat_map.compute_heat_map_overlay_roi`, alpha-composites onto cropped ROI, draws blob ellipses (yellow=walker-selected, cyan=corridor non-winners, gray=raw outside corridor, light-red=motion-gate-rejected), magenta + at prior, dashed acceptance-box on step==0, and audit-mode disagreement marker. Thickness scales with torso height (4%). Color palette sourced from `overlay_config` (no hardcoded hex). Raises loudly on missing frame, missing overlay, or missing required debug fields; driver (WP-3C) handles per-interval containment. Implements WP-3A of the active plan.
- **New active plan [seed_verifier_stage_4b_plan.md](archive/seed_verifier_stage_4b_plan.md).** Plan A landing plan for the Stage 4b seed verifier. Three milestones (M1 per-seed verifier module, M2 downstream consumers, M3 validation), three work packages per M1 and M2, two for M3. Each WP has Depends-on IDs and acceptance criteria. Cross-validation against the blob_walk experiment's startup_label data is the final M3 gate. No contract amendment required.
- **New doc `SEED_VERIFIER.md`.** Doc-only partial delivery of Plan A WP-2c. Describes what the verifier computes, the per-seed JSON schema, the rationale for the downward-extended acceptance box and the 0.7w DoG target, downstream consumer contracts (Stage 4 promotion, UI seed-review tool), regeneration policy, compatibility with missing artifact, and SCHEMA_VERSION versioning. Does not implement the verifier; the implementation lives in the Plan A landing plan.
- **New tool `render_bug_fix_comparison.py`.** Compares `per_variant_summary.csv` against the archived `per_variant_summary_pre_walker_bug_fix.csv` (snapshotted before the walker direction-reversal bug fix) for the 4 affected gate-ON variants. Writes `blob_walk/BUG_FIX_COMPARISON.md` with a per-variant delta table and an interpretation block that branches on the magnitude of V_DEFAULT_RAW's convergent count change to scope Plan C.
- **New tools `tools/blob_walk/{walk_html.py, render_bwd_contact_sheets.py, render_contact_sheets_with_heat.py, render_full_walk.py}`.** Build the per-interval power-of-2 sampled walk view (`blob_walk/walk.html`), render the BWD-direction contact sheets the original batch SCOPE LIMIT omitted, re-render the entire contact-sheet PNG set with a transparent JET heat-map backing under the walker overlays, and emit one PNG per walker row (accepted + soft-miss + hard-reject + past-stop), so every sampled frame produces an image per the 2026-05-25 user direction "render failure is a bug, not a state."
- **`tools/blob_walk/walk.py` emits `obs_cx`, `obs_cy`, `obs_confidence`, `status`, `corridor_cx`, `corridor_cy` on every verdicts row.** Previously the walker only wrote position fields on acceptance; rejected and miss rows had `cx_raw=None` with no record of what the observer saw or why the walker rejected it. The new columns let the contact-sheet renderer draw the candidate ellipse (yellow for accepted, cyan for low-conf, light-red for hard-rejected) and label each tile with its tracking state. `status` values: `accepted`, `soft_miss_low_conf`, `soft_miss_no_blob`, `rejected_max_jump`, `rejected_direction_reversal`. Batch fieldnames updated to write the new columns to `fwd_verdicts.csv` / `bwd_verdicts.csv`.
- **Walker `max_steps` cap dropped: walker now runs all the way to the neighbor seed.** Previously capped at `min(120, distance_to_neighbor_seed)`; 120 frames is 1-2 seconds of video, too short a horizon for low-seed-density videos like Glenbrook (420-frame visible-seed intervals). The walker now terminates only via the natural stop conditions: hit_neighbor_seed (success), miss_cap (soft accumulation), max_jump or direction_reversal (hard gate), boundary. If the walker can reach the merge point it will; if it dies early the existing gates record the reason.
- **`observe_blob_at` gains three opt-in keyword-only override kwargs for seed-local extraction.** `roi_override: tuple | None` bypasses default `_compute_roi` sizing. `dog_diameter_override: float | None` bypasses default DoG diameter tuning. `acceptance_box: tuple | None` bypasses default corridor filter with a rectangular acceptance test. All three default to `None`; with defaults the function is unchanged. When any override is set the per-interval residual cache is bypassed. Production callers (FWD/BWD propagator) use defaults; seed-local walkers use the overrides for tight seed-scoped extraction. Honors contract C1 (seeds never modified by software).

### Behavior or Interface Changes

- **Target-mode CLI shortcuts added in [cli_args.py](../track_runner/cli_args.py).** `target -H` / `target --high` now alias `target -s high`, `target -L` / `target --low` now alias `target -s low`, and `target -A` aliases `target --from-analyze`. The high/low severity shortcuts are mutually exclusive with `-s/--severity` and each other.
- **`track_runner/overlay_styles.yaml` adds `walker_overlays` section for blob walker v2 tile colors.** Seven semantic roles (winner, corridor_non_winner, raw_outside_corridor, rejected_winner, prior_cross, acceptance_box, audit_disagreement) map to hex colors (#FFD000, #06B6D4, #808080, #FF6464, #FF00FF, #FBBF24, #FFFFFF) used by `tools/blob_walk_v2/walk_render.py`. No code hardcodes hex; all colors retrieved from the palette. Configuration-first design allows future color tweaking without code changes.
- **`observe_blob_at` docstring promotes seed-local-walker call shape to first-class status.** Adds explicit two-shape documentation: production-shape (FWD/BWD propagator with default ROI/DoG/corridor, cached) and seed-local-shape (walker with roi_override/dog_diameter_override/acceptance_box, cache-bypassed). Adds function-level comment and cache-bypass section explaining why seed-local queries never share cached blobs with default-shape calls. No behavior change. No signature change.
- **`track_runner.residual_motion.observe_blob_at` gains three opt-in keyword-only override params.** `roi_override: tuple | None = None` replaces the default `_compute_roi` call when set. `dog_diameter_override: float | None = None` replaces `pred_w_p` for the DoG band-pass when set. `acceptance_box: tuple | None = None` replaces the corridor-radius filter with a rectangular acceptance test (blob center must lie inside the box); winner under the box rule is the largest-area blob. All three default to `None`; with defaults the function is byte-identical to production. When any override is set the per-interval residual cache is bypassed (different DoG diameter or ROI would otherwise return wrong cached blobs). Used by `tools/blob_walk/walk.py` bootstrap and `tools/blob_walk/sanity_pack.py`.
- **`BlobObserverTrace` gains three new optional fields: `roi_origin_xy`, `acceptance_box`, `dog_diameter`.** When `trace_sink` is provided to `observe_blob_at`, the trace now captures: (1) `roi_origin_xy` (tuple of top-left ROI corner in processed-frame coords), (2) `acceptance_box` (the acceptance-box override when provided, else None), (3) `dog_diameter` (the actual DoG diameter used in extraction). Existing field `corridor_radius` is now optional with default 0.0. Per-blob enrichment adds: `strength_score`, `size_score`, `proximity_score`, `total_score` on corridor/winner blobs; `in_corridor` (bool) and `in_acceptance_box` (bool or None) on all raw blobs; `dist_to_pred_px` (Euclidean distance to predicted center) on all raw blobs. Trace capture only when `trace_sink is not None`; hot path with `trace_sink=None` is byte-identical to prior behavior.
- **`tools/blob_walk/walk.py` bootstrap now passes seed-local override params to `observe_blob_at`.** DoG target diameter is `0.7 * seed_w`; ROI is the bounding rectangle of the acceptance box padded for the DoG response tail; acceptance box is `[seed_x, seed_y, seed_w, 1.5h]`, extending 0.5h downward in image coordinates toward where motion residual peaks at the runner's thighs. For k >= 1 the walker keeps default `observe_blob_at` behavior. Honors contract C1 (seeds never modified by software).
- **`tools/blob_walk/walk.py` walker `prev` initialization routed through `state_io`-derived seed geometry.** Walker reads `seed['cx']/seed['cy']/seed['w']/seed['h']` directly. The pre-fix walker computed `prev_cx = seed['torso_box'][0]` and `prev_cy = seed['torso_box'][1]`, which are top-left corner pixels, not the center. The bug silently offset every bootstrap by `(w/2, h/2)` toward the upper-left of the runner; for a typical 100 px torso the bootstrap landed roughly 0.5h outside the labeled box. Fix routes all torso-geometry reads through `state_io.load_seeds`, which calls `_derive_seed_geometry` and attaches `cx/cy/w/h` in memory as floats.
- **`tools/blob_walk/config_io.py` loads seeds via `state_io.load_seeds`.** Replaces ad-hoc `json.load` + manual derivation. Returned seeds carry derived `cx/cy/w/h` alongside the raw `torso_box`. New helper `load_race_start_frame(video)` reads `interval_scores.json["pre_race_reference"]["race_start_frame"]` for the pre-race exclusion gate.
- **`tools/blob_walk/batch.py` drops pre-race intervals before sampling.** Any interval whose left endpoint is at or before `race_start_frame` is excluded. Pre-race seeds produce unpredictable blob results because the motion residual has no real runner motion to detect (contract C4 names the pre-race regime as scene-anchored, not runner-motion-anchored). Sampling drop count is logged per video.
- **`tools/blob_walk/batch.py` invokes `hermite_leakage_smoke_test` before the video loop.** The plan named the smoke test a hard blocker, but only the single-walk CLI was wired through to call it; the batch path silently bypassed the gate. Patch adds a one-line invocation with `args = argparse.Namespace(video_basename=videos[0])` before the video loop. PASS at startup on the second rerun; ROI rectangle differs between Hermite and seed-local pred_center as required.
- **`tools/blob_walk/heatmap_png.py` re-composites with a transparent JET overlay over the color source frame.** Production `residual_heat_map.compute_heat_map_roi` renders below-threshold pixels as grayscale (color removed); the walk-tool wrapper now keeps the source frame in color everywhere and adds JET tint only where the residual exceeds the threshold. Calls `compute_heat_map_roi` with `out_arrays={}` to recover the DoG residual + ROI bounds, then composes outside the production helper.
- **`tools/blob_walk/contact_sheet.py` drops the in-PNG legend and scales ellipse / cross / border stroke widths with torso h.** Legend now lives only in `blob_walk/visual.html` (`tools/blob_walk/visual_html.py`). Stroke thicknesses use `max(floor, int(round(fraction * h)))`: thick = 5 percent of h, thin = 3 percent, border = 5 percent. For a 35 px torso this gives ~2-3 px strokes; for a 142 px torso it gives ~7 px strokes. Removes the 160 px legend-canvas extension that was overlaying the runner on tiny-torso PNGs.
- **`tools/blob_walk/overlap_min.write_interval_summary_row` opens the CSV in `mode='w'` instead of `mode='a'`.** Re-runs no longer accumulate stale rows from prior runs. The per-(video, interval, variant) interval-summary CSV is single-row by design.

### Fixes and Maintenance

- **`tools/blob_walk_v2/walk_driver.py` now renders tiles for every sampled offset, not just walker accepts.** Walker `extra_diagnostic_frames` kwarg is wired with power-of-2 offsets across the full interval for both FWD and BWD so the walker emits `after_walk_terminated` rows past stop. Render loops iterate every CSV row (status in accepted/rejected_motion_gate/after_walk_terminated/miss_no_blob/miss_low_conf) and call new helper `_fill_seed_pred` to populate missing `pred_cx/cy/torso_w_px/torso_h_px` from the bracketing seed (left seed for FWD, right seed for BWD). Guarantees a bootstrap (step=0) tile and a first-step tile per interval even when the walker dies at step 1, so the user can see why early termination happened. Fixes "more than half of the frames are missing in blob_walk_v2/walk.html".
- **`tools/blob_walk_v2/walk_html.py` path construction fixed to match driver's on-disk layout.** Builder was looking for tiles under `<run_root>/visual/<video>/seed_<anchor>_<dir>/frame_NNNNNN.png`, but the WP-3C driver writes them at `<run_root>/<video>/seed_<F_L>_<F_R>/{fwd,bwd}/frame_NNNNNN.png`. `_render_column_cells` now takes `interval_dir: pathlib.Path` (the seed_<F_L>_<F_R> directory) and computes `interval_dir/{fwd|bwd}/frame_NNNNNN.png` directly. Discovery loop now requires `interval_summary.csv` to skip half-built interval directories. Missing-PNG behavior softened: CSV-only rows where `status in {soft_miss_no_blob, soft_miss_low_conf, rejected_motion_gate, after_walk_terminated}` OR `pred_cx` is empty (bootstrap step 0) render as a "no_tile" cell with caption-only content and neutral border; LOUD `RuntimeError` is reserved for the real bug shape (status=accepted with non-empty pred_cx, PNG absent). New `.no-tile` CSS class added.
- **Five blob_walk callers reinvented torso geometry; all routed through `state_io.load_seeds`.** The reinvention pattern was the same in each file: read raw JSON, compute center as `torso_box[0]/[1]`, treat as image-coordinate center. The actual center is `(x + w/2, y + h/2)`. Fixed in `tools/blob_walk/walk.py`, `tools/blob_walk/batch.py`, `tools/blob_walk/smoke_test.py`, `tools/blob_walk/sanity_pack.py`, and `tools/test_seed_local_blob_walk.py`. Pattern documented in [TR_DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md](TR_DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md) so new tools do not repeat it.
- **`track_runner.residual_heat_map.compute_heat_map_roi` `fps` parameter is now required (no default).** Previously defaulted to `float(residual_motion.REFERENCE_FPS) = 60`. The default silently produced residuals against wrong neighbor frames on 119.94 fps source video, halving the neighbor temporal spacing and contaminating every blob with non-runner motion. Callers must now pass `fps=reader.fps` explicitly. Docstring updated to name the fragility.
- **Walker direction-reversal gate now uses scene-space displacement on both sides of the dot product.** `tools/blob_walk/walk.py` lines 230-244 previously dotted `walk_accepts[-1][1] - walk_accepts[-2][1]` (PIXEL dx) against `scene_dx` (SCENE dx). The two coordinate spaces agree only on a fixed camera; on panning intervals the gate fired arbitrarily. Patch projects the recent-direction vector into scene coords via `scene_transform.pixel_to_scene` so both vectors live in the same frame. Audit-triggered fix; production `track_runner.velocity_model._apply_blob_snap` is coordinate-consistent and was not changed.
- **Pyflakes-clean sweep on the touched blob_walk modules.** `tools/blob_walk/render_ablation_report.py` dropped three unused imports; `tools/blob_walk/sanity_pack.py` dropped the unused `numpy` import and corrected two missing-placeholder f-strings; `tools/blob_walk/walk.py` removed two dead local assignments left over from the bootstrap rewrite.

### Decisions and Failures

- **Pre-fix walker bootstrap was wrong-runner on Glenbrook seed 1260; root cause was `observe_blob_at` corridor accepting a neighbor's blob.** Default observer returned a blob at (1426, 692.8), 71 px right and 18 px up of the seed center at (1355.5, 710.5) -- a different runner 2.03h horizontally away. The corridor radius `max(1.5w, 0.75h) = 28 px` is wider than the gap between adjacent runners on this footage. Override extraction with the tight acceptance box `[seed_x, seed_y, seed_w, 1.5h]` lands the bootstrap at (1353.4, 719.5), 0.06h from the seed center. The override fix was implemented as additive optional params on `observe_blob_at`, not as a parallel extraction pipeline (per user direction "do not invent new extraction code").
- **Blob walk experiment headline numbers after the second rerun.** Bootstrap accuracy 12.5 percent -> 89.3 percent in_acceptance_box across 56 attempts. Best variant by absolute convergent count: V_NO_MOTION_RAW with 8 of 28 intervals convergent at < 0.5h median FWD/BWD distance (28.6 percent of total, 100 percent of surviving intervals). All four required variants now produce 100 percent convergence on surviving intervals; the differentiator is `surviving / total` (V_NO_MOTION_RAW 28.6 percent vs the other three at 7.1 percent each). Plan decision-tree routing: PROMISING (capped due to 71.4 percent INSUFFICIENT_OVERLAP). User directive: inspect heatmaps before changing the model, since INSUFFICIENT_OVERLAP rate is still high.
- **DoG blob-merging hypothesis REFUTED on the inspected cases.** User suspected the DoG kernel was merging two nearby runners into one blob at the midpoint, explaining the 71-px offset at seed 1260. Trace at three seeds (Glenbrook 1260, Glenbrook 16380, Jason 1222) shows `extract_frame_blobs` returns 6-10 separate blobs per ROI; blobs are NOT merged. Actual cause is "corridor accepts a neighbor blob and rejects the seed-targeted blob," because motion residual peaks at the runner's thighs ~1h below the seed center while the corridor's 0.75h vertical radius is too narrow to include them.
- **Plan B gate ablation: direction-reversal hard-stop is the only load-bearing failure cause.** Four-cell `{motion-term} x {direction-reversal hard-stop}` matrix on the same 5-video corpus shows: both ON 2/28 convergent (default), only direction-reversal OFF 8/28, only motion-term OFF 2/28, both OFF 8/28. The previous V_NO_MOTION_RAW headline benefit (8/28) is entirely attributable to disabling the direction-reversal hard-stop; the motion-term contributes zero rescue. Plan C (Stage 4 single-gate fix) scoped down from "drop both gates" to "drop direction-reversal hard-stop only, keep motion-term scoring as-is." Recorded in `ABLATION_REPORT.md` and reflected in `INTEGRATION_ASSESSMENT.md` Plan C section.
- **Walker direction-reversal gate has coordinate-space bug; production direction gate does not.** `tools/blob_walk/walk.py` line 230 dots `walk_accepts[-1][1] - walk_accepts[-2][1]` (PIXEL displacement) with `scene_dx` (SCENE displacement). The two coordinate spaces only agree when camera motion is zero; on panning intervals the gate can fire spuriously. Production `track_runner/velocity_model.py` `_apply_blob_snap` (lines 820-835) was audited 2026-05-25 and is coordinate-consistent: both `dx = bx - raw_cx` and `v_pred = 0.5 * (v_prev + v_next)` derive from Hermite raw_pred PIXEL coords. The bug is walker-only; production is sound. Consequence: the Plan B ablation result may partly reflect the walker bug rather than the gate's true semantics, so Plan C is demoted from "single-line fix" to "needs validation cycle" (walker bug fix + re-ablate, OR direct production-trace test). Plan A is unaffected since it does not touch the gate.
- **Plan B re-validation after walker bug fix: half the rescue was the bug, half is the gate.** Re-ran the 4 gate-ON variants (V_DEFAULT_RAW, V_NO_SEED_DIR_RAW, V_DEFAULT_POLISH, V_NO_MOTION_TERM_RAW) with the coordinate-space bug fixed. V_DEFAULT_RAW lands at 4/28 convergent (up from 2/28 pre-fix). The fully gate-OFF V_NO_DIR_REVERSAL_RAW still sits at 8/28. The walker bug accounted for roughly half the rescue (4 of 8 intervals); the direction-reversal gate's actual semantics still cost 4 of 8. BOTH contributed. Plan C scope: dropping or relaxing the direction-reversal gate IS still recommended, but the benefit is half what Plan B originally measured, and the production gate must be re-tested directly because the walker test was contaminated. Details in `BUG_FIX_COMPARISON.md`.

### Developer Tests and Notes

- **Target parser shortcut tests added in [test_tr_target_mode.py](../tests/test_tr_target_mode.py).** Focused coverage verifies `target -H`, `target --low`, `target -A`, and conflicting high/low severity aliases.
- **Two batch runs archived for evidence retention.** `blob_walk_pre_seed_geometry_fix/` holds the original run before the top-left-as-center bug was fixed (bootstrap in_box 12.5 percent). `blob_walk_pre_override_fix/` holds the run after the geometry fix but before the seed-frame override extraction was added. Both retain their REPORT.md, per_variant_summary.csv, contact sheets, and heat-map MP4s. The current `blob_walk/` directory holds the post-override results delivered with `INTEGRATION_ASSESSMENT.md`.

## 2026-05-24

### Additions and New Features

- **New audit doc [cli_argument_audit.md](archive/cli_argument_audit.md).** Full inventory of every CLI flag across the eight track_runner subcommands (`seed`, `edit`, `target`, `solve`, `refine`, `encode`, `analyze`, `setup`) plus the global parser. Each row labeled OBSERVED / PROPOSED / APPROVED. Findings F1-F12 enumerate single-letter collisions (`-s` severity vs `--seed`), missing short flags on routinely-toggled encode options, asymmetric prompt-mutex coverage, and the undocumented uppercase-short-flag rule. Closeout patch of `~/.claude/plans/distributed-stirring-church.md` Milestone 1.
- **New decision record [cli_unification_decisions.md](archive/cli_unification_decisions.md).** D1-D9 status table. D1-D5 and D7-D9 APPROVED on 2026-05-23; D6 (rename deprecation policy) DEFERRED to the follow-up rename plan with default = hard-cut. Closeout patch of `~/.claude/plans/distributed-stirring-church.md` Milestone 2.
- **New validation artifact `cli_argparse_dump.txt`.** Concatenated `--help` output for the global parser and all eight subparsers, dumped from the live argparse tree. Cross-checked against the audit doc's OBSERVED inventory rows. Regenerate with `source source_me.sh && python3 tools/dump_cli_help.py > docs/active_plans/audits/cli_argparse_dump.txt`.
- **New tool [dump_cli_help.py](../tools/dump_cli_help.py).** Imports `track_runner.cli_args._build_parser()` and writes the global + per-subparser `--help` text to stdout. Used to produce the audit's validation artifact without triggering required-input validation.

### Behavior or Interface Changes

- **`track_runner/cli_args.parse_args` refactored: parser construction split into `_build_parser()`.** New public-ish `_build_parser()` returns the fully-configured `ArgumentParser` tree; `parse_args()` is now a thin wrapper that calls `_build_parser().parse_args()`. Behavior unchanged for normal CLI invocation. Enables the new `tools/dump_cli_help.py` to walk subparsers without triggering required-input validation.

### Decisions and Failures

- **Published CLI argument audit; reconciliation APPROVED for D1-D5, D7-D9.** Unified single-letter scheme adopted: drop `-s` short on analyze (resolves the severity-vs-seed collision); broad short-flag additions on encode (`-a/--aspect`, `-m/--mp4`, `-q/--crf`, `-v/--video-codec`, `-l/--draw-tracking`, `-D/--draw-debug`, `-V/--draw-velocity-arrow`, `-Z/--torso-multiple`); drop `-c` short on `--config`; drop `--no-filters` (use `-F none`); remove `--keep-temp`; rename `--debug` long form to `--debug-logs`; add `-k/--keep`, `-u/--upgrade`, `-b/--bin`, `-B/--auto-bin`, `-T/--time-range`. Behavior change: `--auto-bin` default-on at target 480; `--bin 1` becomes the explicit opt-out (D9). Move `seed_interval=10.0` default to config (D4). D6 (deprecation policy) deferred to the follow-up rename plan; default = hard-cut.
- **No production code renamed by this audit; no test changes.** Code edits land in a follow-up rename plan. Argparse-inventory pytests intentionally not added: assertions on collections of flag names are fragile per [PYTEST_STYLE.md](PYTEST_STYLE.md). Inventory parity stays a doc-review checklist.

## 2026-05-23

### Additions and New Features

- **New module [blob_trace.py](../track_runner/blob_trace.py).** Pure dataclass module that defines `BlobObserverTrace` and `BlobGateTrace` for the opt-in observer/gate diagnostic side-channel used by the visual-audit tooling. Closeout patch of `~/.claude/plans/kind-exploring-cray.md`.
- **New tool `check_heatmap_equivalence.py`.** Sample-frame comparator that runs the user-visible heat-map render path against the solver-internal `compute_heat_map_roi` and reports `max_abs_diff` across sampled frames. Used by M0.5 to verify the user's "blob is obvious" visual intuition is the same array the solver consults; produced an EQUIVALENT verdict (six frames sampled on the Jason video, `max_abs_diff = 0.0`).
- **New tool `seed_oracle_blob_audit.py`.** Leave-one-out (LOO) seed-oracle harness for M0.7. For each held-out seed, resolves the bracketing interval without it, replays the observer at the held-out frame, and writes per-seed CSV rows that include H1 mask-overlap and H8 ROI-relative coordinates so H1/H2/H3 hypotheses can be tested.
- **New tool `test_blob_hypotheses.py`.** Aggregator that reads the LOO oracle CSV and reports verdicts on the three working hypotheses (H1 mask-overlap, H2 raw_pred-systematically-off, H3 closest-raw-blob-within-tolerance). Preliminary verdicts from a five-seed IMG_4005 smoke: H2 REFUTED (raw_pred is not systematically off), H1 INCONCLUSIVE (sample too small), H3 INCONCLUSIVE_SCHEMA (closest_raw_blob column gap noted for the next fix cycle).
- **New tool `visualize_blob_gates.py`.** Visual diagnostic that renders per-frame six-panel layouts of the propagator's blob-gate inputs (heat-map ROI, DoG, validity mask, raw_pred, accepted blob, gate verdict). Supports single-frame mode, interval mode (range of frames + HTML index), and a funnel-stage classifier CSV (`lost_at_gate_prox`, `lost_at_gate_dir`, `lost_at_gate_smooth`, `accepted`). M2 funnel-stage smoke on the TRUST 0% interval 2444-2491 FWD pass reported 43 of 46 non-endpoint frames (~93 percent) lost at the proximity gate -- the observer finds blobs and the corridor keeps them, but the blob sits more than 0.6 * h from `raw_pred`.
- **New tool `classify_blob_failure_mode.py`.** M3 funnel-stage classifier that consumes the visualize_blob_gates CSV and bins each frame's failure mode into the canonical labels above for reviewer triage.
- **New tool `blob_distance_histogram.py`.** WP-2A test-4 analyzer for `~/.claude/plans/kind-exploring-cray.md`. Walks every `verdicts.csv` matched by `--verdicts-glob`, filters to `lost_at_stage == "gate_prox"` rows, and bins `winner_dist_h` into 30 width-0.1 bins over [MARKDOWN_STYLE.md](MARKDOWN_STYLE.md); no Unicode block characters). The corpus mode bin decides H_DIST_SHAPE_THRESHOLD (mode in `test_alternate_blob_winner.py`.** WP-2B alternate-blob test for `~/.claude/plans/kind-exploring-cray.md`. Walks every `verdicts.csv` matched by `--verdicts-glob`, filters to `lost_at_stage == "gate_prox"` rows, parses the `corridor_blobs_json` column (per-blob `cx, cy, area, dist_h, integrated_mag, size_score, proximity_score, total_score`), finds the minimum-`dist_h` blob, and counts how many would pass the current proximity gate (`dist_h <= 0.6`). Emits per-interval, per-video, and corpus-wide rescue rates plus the H_ALT_PASSES verdict (SUPPORTED when corpus rescue rate >= 0.50; REFUTED below; INCONCLUSIVE on empty samples). Output is a markdown report. No real verdicts data yet; tool verified via fixture pytest only.
- **New tool `blob_fix_suspension_check.py`.** WP-1D plan-suspension pre-check for `~/.claude/plans/kind-exploring-cray.md`. Reads `<run-root>/oracle/HYPOTHESIS_TESTS.json` (machine-readable sidecar, NOT the Markdown report) and decides H_RAW_PRED_CORPUS at the 30% threshold corpus-wide AND across `>= 6` of the 12 per-video H2 entries (per-video SUPPORTED-at-30% means `rate >= 0.30`). Emits `SUSPENSION_CHECK.md` with two canonical machine-readable verdict lines (`H_RAW_PRED_CORPUS: SUPPORTED|REFUTED|INCONCLUSIVE` and `Overall: PROCEED|PLAN_SUSPENDED`) plus a per-video H2-rate table for transparency. CLI: `-r/--run-root` defaults to `output_smoke/blob_refine_fix/<latest_date>`, `-o/--output` defaults to `<run-root>/oracle/SUSPENSION_CHECK.md`. Override path is human-only: a reviewer may later add `PROCEED_OVERRIDE: <reason>` to allow downstream consumers to proceed on PLAN_SUSPENDED; the tool itself does not parse or write overrides.
- **New tool `test_centroid_bias.py`.** WP-2C centroid-bias analyzer for `~/.claude/plans/kind-exploring-cray.md`. Consumes the WP-1A leave-one-out oracle CSV, filters rows with `loo_winner_dist_h > 0.6`, and cross-tabs `loo_winner_mask_overlap_frac >= 0.5` against the centroid-far flag on the TRUST 0% subset to decide H_CENTROID_BIAS (Test 6: SUPPORTED at >= 40% corpus rate AND >= 6 of 12 videos at >= 40%). Also runs the policy-E shifted-centroid oracle-correctness checks (AC-2d on TRUST 0%, AC-2e on positive controls; PASS when mean delta of `dist(shifted, seed_torso) - dist(loo_winner, seed_torso)` is strictly negative, with `shifted = (loo_winner + raw_pred_loo) / 2`). Emits per-video + corpus markdown report plus the sidecar `analyses/policy_e_gates.json` consumed by WP-3A (counterfactual_gate_sim.py) for the policy-E inclusion decision (`h1_supported_corpus`, `h1_supported_videos_count`, `oracle_correctness_trust_0_pass`, `oracle_correctness_pos_ctrl_pass`, `policy_e_eligible`).
- **New tool `counterfactual_gate_sim.py`.** WP-3A skeleton for `~/.claude/plans/kind-exploring-cray.md`. CLI accepts `-c/--oracle-csv` (WP-1A oracle.csv), `-g/--verdicts-glob` (WP-1B verdicts.csv glob), `-e/--policy-e-gates` (WP-2C policy_e_gates.json), and `-o/--output-dir` (RANKING.md + by_policy.csv + per_video_policy.csv + ranking.json). Imports the production blob-snap constants from `track_runner.velocity_model` (BLOB_SNAP_ALPHA, BLOB_SNAP_VELOCITY_FLOOR, BLOB_SNAP_MAX_SHIFT_FRACTION, BLOB_SNAP_ALPHA_MAX, BLOB_SNAP_PATH_SLACK, BLOB_SNAP_PATH_PERP_FRACTION) so AC-3b byte-fidelity holds against live constants; intentionally does NOT import `track_runner.residual_motion` (AC-3l). Static policy set `POLICIES = {"A", "B", "C", "F", "G"}`; policy E joins conditionally based on `policy_e_eligible`. Skeleton scope: policy A replay IS implemented end-to-end (proximity test recomputed from BLOB_SNAP_ALPHA, direction-dot gate honoring BLOB_SNAP_VELOCITY_FLOOR, `path_ok_prev` read from the verdicts row per plan scope choice); policies B / C / F / G / E raise `NotImplementedError` from `replay_policy()`. Aggregator and G-4 evaluator are stubs; the G-4 evaluator hard-codes REJECT-all so M4 cannot consume the skeleton ranking by accident. Emits skeleton RANKING.md (declares skeleton state + AC-3b replay rate), empty by_policy.csv / per_video_policy.csv headers, and ranking.json with `{"skeleton": true, "policies_evaluated": [...]}`.
- **New audit doc [blob_refinement_visual_audit.md](archive/blob_problem_discovery/blob_refinement_visual_audit.md).** PRELIMINARY audit record covering M0-M4 outcomes: production trace path, heat-map equivalence verdict, LOO seed-oracle audit, visual diagnostic, interval/contact-sheet/HTML index, funnel-stage classifier, and consolidated findings for the next reviewer cycle.
- **New tool `build_interval_selection.py`.** WP-1B Step 1 selection-CSV emitter for `~/.claude/plans/kind-exploring-cray.md`. Walks `tr_config/*.track_runner.interval_scores.json` (12 videos) and emits `output_smoke/blob_refine_fix/2026-05-23/m2_sweep/per_video_selection.csv` with up to 7 ranking rows per video (5 TRUST + 2 WEAK/FAIR) and up to 2 held-out rows per video (1 + 1). Tier mapping uses only the tier names present in `interval_score.confidence_tier`: TRUST = `high`, WEAK/FAIR = `low` or `fair`; `good` and `pre_race` are ignored for selection. Random draw uses a single shared `numpy.random.default_rng(20260523)` instance shuffling each per-video pool; the ranking slice (`[:quota]`) and held-out slice (`[quota:quota+1]`) come from the same shuffle so the two samples are disjoint by construction. CSV columns are locked to `rng_seed, sample, video, interval_start, interval_end, bucket, tier, agreement_score, blob_accept_fwd, blob_accept_bwd` so the WP-1C `tools/per_video_report.py` reader continues to consume the same header. The `bucket` column carries the placeholders `trust_pending` / `weak_fair_pending` and both `blob_accept_*` columns carry the literal `pending_sweep` string -- `interval_scores.json` does not record per-interval blob-accept counts, so the final TRUST 0% / TRUST high-accept / Mixed / WEAK low-accept buckets defined in plan section "Per-video discovery and held-out sampling" are resolved by a separate post-sweep rebucket step (not built here). Real-data run on the current 12-video `tr_config/` corpus produced 96 rows (74 ranking, 22 held-out; 60 trust_pending, 36 weak_fair_pending). READ-ONLY on `tr_config/`; the only write target is `--output`, which defaults to `output_smoke/blob_refine_fix/2026-05-23/m2_sweep/per_video_selection.csv`.
- **New fixture-driven pytest `test_build_interval_selection.py` for WP-1B Step 1.** Eight tests on a hand-built two-video tmp_path fixture (video A: 8 TRUST + 4 WEAK/FAIR + 1 good + 1 pre_race; video B: 2 TRUST + 1 WEAK/FAIR for the pool-smaller-than-quota path). Asserts the locked CSV header (`build_interval_selection.CSV_FIELDNAMES`), that the RNG seed is recorded in every row, that ranking and held-out samples are disjoint per `(video, interval_start, interval_end)`, that per-video quotas are honored as upper bounds for both tiers in both samples, that an over-subscribed pool fills ranking and leaves held-out empty, that bucket and blob-accept placeholder strings are exactly the documented values, that `good`/`pre_race` intervals never appear in the CSV, and that two runs with the same seed produce byte-identical CSVs. Pyflakes clean; 8/8 GREEN.
- **New tool `per_video_report.py`.** WP-1C per-video rollup writer for `~/.claude/plans/kind-exploring-cray.md`. Reads `<run-root>/m2_sweep/per_video_selection.csv`, every per-interval `<run-root>/m2_sweep/<video>/interval_*/verdicts.csv` and `CLASSIFICATION.md`, `<run-root>/oracle/oracle.csv`, and `<run-root>/oracle/HYPOTHESIS_TESTS.json` (the WP-1A `{corpus, per_video}` sidecar schema). Emits one `<output-dir>/{basename}.md` per video with sections for Header (seed count, race-start frame, total intervals from optional `tr_config/`), TRUST 0% intervals (current `blob_accept_fwd / blob_accept_bwd`, dominant `lost_at_stage` tag, top-3 stage percents per pass, per-interval torso-h relative jitter `std(raw_box.h[t] - raw_box.h[t-1]) / median(raw_box.h)` on interior frames), Positive-control intervals (same fields), Mixed intervals (same), `winner_dist_h` histogram summary (mode bin + count), Hypothesis verdicts at the 30% threshold (H1, H2, H6, H8 from the per-video JSON block), and a one-line torso-h jitter summary (`trust_0_jitter_mean`, `pos_ctrl_jitter_mean`). Also writes `corpus_index.md` (linking every per-video file plus a corpus-wide summary table) and the `per_video_summary.csv` sidecar with the documented columns (`video, trust_0_count, pos_ctrl_count, mixed_count, dominant_lost_stage_trust_0, dominant_lost_stage_pos_ctrl, h1_supported, h2_supported, h6_supported, h8_supported, trust_0_jitter_mean, pos_ctrl_jitter_mean, winner_dist_h_mode_bin_low`). CLI: `-r/--run-root` defaults to `output_smoke/blob_refine_fix/<latest_date>`, `-o/--output-dir` defaults to `<run-root>/per_video_reports/`, optional `-c/--tr-config-dir` for header lookups. Skeleton pass: tool consumes the documented schemas. No real verdicts data yet; tool verified via fixture pytest only.

### Behavior or Interface Changes

- **`tools/test_blob_hypotheses.py` JSON sidecar now emits the WP-1A `{corpus, per_video}` schema.** Previously the JSON wrote a flat `hypotheses` list keyed only at corpus scope; downstream consumers (WP-1D, WP-2C, WP-3A policy E gate) expect the per-video block. The analyzer now reruns the five hypothesis tests (H1, H2, H3, H6, H8) on each `df['video'].unique()` slice and writes `{"corpus": {hN: {verdict, rate, n_eligible}}, "per_video": {video: {hN: ...}}}`. The Markdown report (`HYPOTHESIS_TESTS.md`) is unchanged; only the JSON serialization moved. `HypothesisResult` dataclass gained `key`, `n_eligible`, and `rate_primary` fields so per-scope verdict blocks read from the same struct.
- **`track_runner.residual_motion.observe_blob_at` gains opt-in `trace_sink` kwarg.** Default `None`; when supplied, the function populates a `BlobObserverTrace` instance for the call. With `trace_sink=None` the function is byte-identical to today (no extra allocations on the hot path). Used by `tools/visualize_blob_gates.py` and `tools/seed_oracle_blob_audit.py` to inspect observer internals from outside the solver without modifying solver behavior.
- **`track_runner.velocity_model._apply_blob_snap` gains opt-in `trace_sink` kwarg.** Default `None`; when supplied, captures per-frame proximity/direction/smoothness gate outcomes into a `BlobGateTrace`. With `trace_sink=None`, byte-identical to today.
- **`track_runner.residual_heat_map.compute_heat_map_roi` gains opt-in `out_arrays` kwarg.** Default `None`; when supplied, returns the intermediate residual + DoG + validity-mask arrays as a diagnostic side-channel for the M0.5 equivalence comparator. With `out_arrays=None`, byte-identical to today.
- **Per-interval residual cache in `observe_blob_at` extended to hold `residual_pre_dog`.** The interval-scoped cache now stores `residual_pre_dog` alongside the existing `residual_dog` and `validity_mask` entries. Only raw image-derived data is cached, preserving the C-anti-pattern rule against accepted-blob / gate-outcome / `snap_pred` carry-over between frames.

### Decisions and Failures

- **Heat-map equivalence verified at the array level (six frames on Jason, `max_abs_diff = 0.0`).** The audit confirms the user-visible blob-cue render path produces byte-identical arrays to the solver-internal `compute_heat_map_roi`. "Blob is obvious" reflects the same data the solver gates consult; the failure mode lives downstream of array equivalence (gate thresholds, not heat-map content). See [blob_refinement_visual_audit.md](archive/blob_problem_discovery/blob_refinement_visual_audit.md).
- **Pre-known "validity-mask ordering divergence" suspicion (heat_map AFTER DoG vs observer BEFORE) REFUTED by code re-read.** Both code paths apply the validity mask AFTER DoG. The plan was updated to drop this candidate before any further audit cycles. The named falsified arrow is "validity-mask ordering divergence between render and observer."
- **WP-0.7A oracle CSV ran twice through reviewer cycles.** First fix wired the LOO replay; the final fix-2 additionally wired the H1 mask-overlap column and the H8 ROI-relative coordinates so future hypothesis tests do not require another re-run of the LOO sweep.
- **M2 smoke verdict on TRUST 0% interval 2444-2491 FWD.** 43/46 non-endpoint frames (~93 percent) lose at the proximity gate; observer finds blobs and corridor keeps them, but the blob is more than 0.6 * h from `raw_pred`. This is the next reviewer-cycle's primary finding.

Asserts the JSON top-level keys are exactly `{corpus, per_video}`, that `corpus.h2` carries `{verdict, rate, n_eligible}`, that all five hypothesis keys (`h1, h2, h3, h6, h8`) appear in both corpus and every per-video block, that per_video contains one entry per video, and that H8 eligibility on the fixture matches all six rows (h_seed > 0 with pre/post DoG values). Invokes the tool via `subprocess.run` so the markdown / JSON dual-emit path is end-to-end exercised. Pyflakes clean; 5/5 GREEN.
- **New fixture-driven pytest `test_blob_distance_histogram.py` for WP-2A.** Twelve tests on a hand-built tmp_path verdicts.csv layout matching the plan's `m2_sweep/<video>/interval_<start>_<end>/verdicts.csv` shape. Asserts bin-index extraction at boundary values (0.7 -> bin 7, 1.8 -> bin 18) despite the binary-float `0.7/0.1 == 6.9999...` trap, overflow bin assignment for `>= 3.0`, classification verdicts across the THRESHOLD / BLOB / INCONCLUSIVE bands, end-to-end mode extraction on the two-row prox-rejection fixture (`0.7h` and `1.8h`), corpus aggregation across per-interval / per-video / corpus-wide groups, that the Markdown report names the corpus verdict and uses `#` bars per [MARKDOWN_STYLE.md](MARKDOWN_STYLE.md), and that filter rules drop non-`gate_prox` rows.
